d) acquires or keeps a medicine or veterinary medicinal product not authorized in Hungary in an unjustified quantity, brings it into the territory of the country, takes it out of it, transports it through it,

e) uses an original document related to a medicine or veterinary medicinal product for commercial purposes in a manner contrary to its intended purpose, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

(2) * If the offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed with regard to a medicine containing a psychotropic substance listed in schedule 3 or 4 of psychotropic substances in Annex 2 of Government Decree 78/2022 (XII. 28.) of the Ministry of Interior on controlled substances [hereinafter: BM Decree 78/2022 (XII. 28.)], or with regard to the active ingredient of such medicine in violation of the notification obligation, without authorization or exceeding the limits of authorization, the punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years.

(3) The punishment in the cases defined in paragraph (1) points a) and b) is

a) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense causes permanent disability or serious deterioration of health,

b) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense causes death.

(4) The punishment in the case defined in paragraph (2) is

a) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense according to paragraph (1) point a) or b) causes permanent disability or serious deterioration of health,

b) imprisonment from five years to ten years if the offense according to paragraph (1) point a) or b) causes death.

(5) Whoever commits the offense

a) as a healthcare worker,

b) as an employee of an organization authorized to manufacture, wholesale, or provide direct supply to the public, or

c) in a criminal association, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years in the case defined in paragraph (1), and by imprisonment from two years to eight years in the case defined in paragraph (2).

(6) It shall be punishable according to paragraph (5) if the false, falsified or medicine or veterinary medicinal product not authorized in Hungary becomes widely accessible to users.

(7) Whoever commits preparatory acts aimed at medicine falsification defined in paragraphs (1)-(6) shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(8) For the purposes of this Section

a) unjustified quantity means a quantity which can be determined not to be intended to satisfy the personal needs of a specific person,

b) medicine or veterinary medicinal product not authorized in Hungary also means a product in which a medicinal active ingredient is used in violation of the legal requirements for the composition of the product in question. A medicine without marketing authorization in Hungary must also be considered as an authorized medicine with respect to which a conduct defined in paragraph (1) point b) or d) is carried out that can be lawfully performed with the required official authorization or after notification.

## 186. § (1) Whoever

a) falsifies a medical device, or produces a false medical device,

b) offers, transfers, places on the market, or trades in a false, falsified or medical device not authorized in Hungary,

c) brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, transports through it a false or falsified medical device, or acquires or keeps it in an unjustified quantity,

d) acquires or keeps a medical device not authorized in Hungary in an unjustified quantity, brings it into the territory of the country, takes it out of it, transports it through it,

e) uses an original document related to a medical device for commercial purposes in a manner contrary to its intended purpose, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

(2) The punishment in the cases defined in paragraph (1) points a) and b) is

a) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense causes permanent disability or serious deterioration of health,

b) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense causes death.

(3) Whoever commits the offense defined in paragraph (1)

a) as a healthcare worker,

b) as an employee of an organization authorized to manufacture, wholesale, or provide direct supply to the public, or

c) in a criminal association, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years.

(4) It shall be punishable according to paragraph (3) if the false, falsified or medical device not authorized in Hungary becomes widely accessible to users.

(4a) * Whoever commits preparatory acts aimed at the falsification of medical devices defined in paragraphs (1)-(4) shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(5) For the purposes of this Section

a) * medical device means: a medical device, an in vitro diagnostic medical device and an investigational medicinal product;

b) unjustified quantity means a quantity which can be determined not to be intended to satisfy the personal needs of a specific person;

c) * medical device not authorized in Hungary also means a medical device placed on the market without conducting a conformity assessment procedure.

## 187. § (1) * Anyone who, for consideration or regularly,

a) engages in medical practice,

b) *

c) unlawfully engages in activities falling within the scope of non-conventional healing and naturopathic procedures, shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to one year for a misdemeanor.

(1a) * Shall be punishable according to paragraph (1), anyone who provides health services for consideration or regularly, unlawfully presenting themselves as a psychotherapist.

(2) The penalty for a felony is imprisonment for up to three years if quackery is committed by feigning authorization to practice medicine.

(3) For the purposes of this Section, a person authorized to practice medicine is:

a) a person who has a general medical or dental diploma obtained at a domestic university,

b) a person who has a general medical or dental diploma obtained at a foreign university, naturalized and provided with an equivalence clause,

c) a foreign citizen who, on the basis of a permit, may engage in medical activities without naturalization of their diploma, provided they are not subject to a prohibition from medical activity.

(4) The perpetrator of quackery may also be subject to expulsion.

## 188. §

(1) Anyone who unlawfully prepares, possesses, uses or places poison on the market, or who fails to take the measures prescribed to prevent the abusive use of poisons or to exclude the endangerment of other persons, if a more serious crime is not committed, shall be punishable by confinement for a misdemeanor.

(2) For the purposes of this Section, poison means a hazardous substance or mixture which, on the basis of its toxicological properties, endangers human life or may cause serious injury or serious harm to health if inhaled, swallowed or absorbed through the skin.

## 189. §

(1) Anyone who prepares or possesses consumer goods harmful to health for the purpose of placing them on the market shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to two years for a misdemeanor.

(2) Anyone who places harmful consumer goods on the market shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years for a felony.

(3) The penalty is imprisonment from one to five years if the crime is committed

a) against a significant quantity or value of harmful consumer goods,

b) in a criminal conspiracy, or

c) as a business.

(4) Anyone who commits the crime defined in paragraph (2) by negligence shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to one year for a misdemeanor.

(5) The perpetrator of the crime defined in paragraphs (1) and (4) shall not be punishable if, as soon as they become aware of the harmful nature of the consumer goods, they do everything to ensure that the harmful consumer goods are returned to their possession or are not placed on the market.

## 190. § (1) Anyone who deprives another of their personal liberty

a) by violence or direct threat against life or physical integrity, or

b) by placing them in a state incapable of defense or declaration of will or by exploiting such a state, and makes their release dependent on the fulfillment of a demand, shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years for a felony.

(2) The penalty is imprisonment from five to fifteen years if the kidnapping is committed

a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,

b) in a criminal conspiracy,

c) with a weapon,

d) armed, or

e) against an official person or foreign official person, with regard to this capacity.

(3) The penalty is imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment if the kidnapping is committed

a) against a person who has not reached the age of fourteen,

b) causing particularly serious harm, or

c) causing death.

(4) The penalty is imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment if the kidnapping also constitutes intentional homicide.

(5) Anyone who commits preparatory acts for kidnapping shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years.

(6) The punishment may be mitigated without limitation for anyone who voluntarily abandons the kidnapping before serious consequences result from it.

## 191. §

Anyone who obtains credible knowledge that a kidnapping is being prepared and does not inform the person concerned or the authorities as soon as possible, if the kidnapping is attempted or committed, shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years for a felony.

## 192. § * (1) Anyone who

a) sells, purchases, exchanges, transfers or receives another as consideration, or

b) transports, accommodates, conceals, or obtains another for another person for the purpose of committing the act defined in point (a), shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years for a felony.

(2) Anyone who, for the purpose of obtaining regular advantage, induces another to work, work-like activity, other service, or the continuation of unlawful acts

a) by deception, by abusing a power or influence relationship related to the education, supervision, care, medical treatment of the victim, or any other power or influence relationship with the victim, or by exploiting the victim's vulnerable situation, or

b) coerces by violence or threat, shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years for a felony.

(3) Anyone who, for the purpose of committing the act defined in paragraph (2), recruits, transfers, receives, sells, purchases, exchanges, transfers or receives as consideration, obtains for another, transports, accommodates, conceals another, shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years for a felony.

(4) Anyone who commits the crime defined in paragraph (2) or (3) for the purpose of sexual acts or prohibited use of the human body shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years for a felony.

(5) The penalty in the case of the crime defined in paragraph (2) or (3) is imprisonment from five to ten years, in the case of the crime defined in paragraph (4) imprisonment from five to fifteen years, if it is committed

a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,

b) with the maltreatment of the victim,

c) as an official person, exploiting this capacity, or

d) against multiple persons.

(6) The penalty in the case of the crime defined in paragraph (2) or (3) is imprisonment from five to fifteen years, in the case of the crime defined in paragraph (4) imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment, if it is committed

a) against a person who has not reached the age of twelve, or

b) causing danger to life or particularly serious harm.

(7) Anyone who commits preparatory acts for human trafficking and forced labor, in the case defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to one year for a misdemeanor, in the case defined in paragraph (2) or (3) by imprisonment for up to three years for a felony, in the case defined in paragraph (4) by imprisonment from one to five years.

(8) Anyone who

a) uses or exploits the work, work-like activity, other service or unlawful act of the victim of human trafficking and forced labor defined in paragraph (2), shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years,

b) commits the crime defined in point (a) by using sexual acts or for the prohibited use of the human body, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.

## 193. § *

## 194. §

(1) Anyone who deprives another of their personal liberty shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years for a felony.

(2) The penalty is imprisonment from one to five years if the violation of personal liberty is committed

a) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,

b) for a base motive or purpose,

c) with the maltreatment of the victim,

d) against a person incapable of defense,

e) with a weapon,

f) armed,

g) causing significant harm to interests, or

h) by feigning official proceedings.

(3) The penalty is imprisonment from two to eight years if the crime committed against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen also qualifies according to point (b) or (c) of paragraph (2).

## 195. §

Whoever coerces another person by force or threat to do, not do, or tolerate something, and thereby causes significant harm to interests, if no other criminal offense is committed, is punishable by imprisonment for a felony for up to three years.

## 196. §

(1) Whoever coerces another person to perform or tolerate a sexual act is punishable by imprisonment for a felony from one year to five years.

(2) Punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years is the person who commits sexual coercion

a) against a person who has not reached eighteen years of age,

b) against their relative, or a person under their education, supervision, care, or medical treatment, or by abusing another position of power or influence over the victim.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years if the sexual coercion is committed against a person who has not reached fourteen years of age.

## 197. § (1) Commits sexual violence, and is punishable by imprisonment for a felony from two years to eight years, the person who

a) commits sexual coercion by force or by direct threat against life or physical integrity,

b) exploits another person's state of incapacity to defend themselves or express their will for a sexual act.

(2) Also commits sexual violence, and is punishable by imprisonment from five years to ten years, the person who performs or causes to be performed a sexual act with a person who has not reached twelve years of age.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years if the offense defined in subsection (1)

a) is committed against a person who has not reached eighteen years of age,

b) is committed by the perpetrator against their relative, or a person under their education, supervision, care, medical treatment, or otherwise under their power or influence, or

c) is committed by multiple persons on the same occasion, with knowledge of each other's actions.

(4) * Punishable by imprisonment from five years to twenty years,

a) the person who commits the offense defined in subsection (2) aa) in the manner defined in subsection (1), ab) against the victim defined in subsection (3) point b), or ac) in the manner defined in subsection (3) point c), or

b) if the offense defined in subsection (3) point a) also qualifies under subsection (3) point b) or c).

(4a) * The punishment is imprisonment from ten years to twenty years if the offense defined in subsection (4) point a) subpoint aa) also qualifies under subsection (4) point a) subpoint ab) or ac).

(5) Whoever provides the conditions necessary for or facilitating the commission of sexual violence is punishable by imprisonment for a felony for up to three years.

## 198. §

(1) A person who has reached eighteen years of age who performs a sexual act with a person who has not reached fourteen years of age, or induces such person to perform a sexual act with another, is punishable by imprisonment for a felony from one year to five years.

(2) A person who has reached eighteen years of age who attempts to induce a person who has not reached fourteen years of age to perform a sexual act with them or with another is punishable by imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) If the victim is the perpetrator's relative or is under their education, supervision, care, or medical treatment, or the perpetrator commits the sexual abuse by abusing another position of power or influence over the victim, the punishment is

a) in the case defined in subsection (1) from two years to eight years,

b) in the case defined in subsection (2) imprisonment from one year to five years.

(4) A person who has reached eighteen years of age who performs a sexual act with a person who has reached fourteen years of age but has not reached eighteen years of age by abusing a position of power or influence over them is punishable by imprisonment for up to three years.

## 199. §

(1) Whoever performs a sexual act with their lineal relative is punishable by imprisonment for a felony for up to three years.

(2) Whoever has sexual intercourse with their sibling is punishable by imprisonment for a misdemeanor for up to two years.

(3) The descendant is not punishable for the offense defined in subsection (1) if they had not reached eighteen years of age at the time of committing the act.

## 200. §

(1) Whoever procures someone for another for the performance of a sexual act for the purpose of gaining profit is punishable by imprisonment for a felony for up to three years.

(2) * Commits procuring, and is punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years, the person who offers or solicits a person who has not reached eighteen years of age for prostitution.

(3) * The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if the procuring is committed as a business.

(3a) * If the offense defined in subsections (1)–(3) is committed as a special recidivist, the punishment is

a) in the case defined in subsection (1) from one year to five years,

b) in the case defined in subsection (2) from two years to eight years,

c) in the case defined in subsection (3) imprisonment from five years to ten years.

(4) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if the procuring defined in subsection (1)

a) is committed against a person who has not reached eighteen years of age,

b) is committed by the perpetrator against their relative, a person under their education, supervision or care, or by abusing another position of power or influence over the victim,

c) is committed by deception, force or threat.

(5) * The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years if

a) the procuring defined in subsection (4) point a) or b) also qualifies under point c),

b) the procuring defined in any point of subsection (4) is committed as a special recidivist.

(6) Whoever agrees to commit procuring as a business is punishable by imprisonment for up to three years.

## 201. § (1) Whoever

a) induces another person to engage in prostitution,

b) makes available a building or other place to another for the purpose of prostitution,

c) * is punishable by imprisonment for a felony for up to three years.

(2) * Whoever commits the offense defined in subsection (1) in connection with the prostitution of a person who has not reached eighteen years of age, or provides assistance to the prostitution of a person who has not reached eighteen years of age, is punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.

(3) Whoever maintains, operates a brothel, or provides financial means for its operation, is punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years.

(4) * If the facilitation of prostitution is committed as a special recidivist, the punishment is

a) in the case defined in subsection (1) from one year to five years,

b) in the case defined in subsection (2) from five years to ten years,

c) in the case defined in subsection (3) imprisonment from two years to eight years.

## 202. §

Whoever has themselves wholly or partly supported by a person engaged in prostitution is punishable by imprisonment for a felony for up to three years.

## 203. §

(1) * Whoever seeks to gain profit from the prostitution of a person who has not reached eighteen years of age is punishable by imprisonment for a felony from two years to eight years.

(2) * Whoever provides compensation for a sexual act with a person who has not reached eighteen years of age is punishable by imprisonment for a felony from two years to eight years.

(3) * Whoever has themselves partly or wholly supported by a person who has not reached eighteen years of age engaged in prostitution is punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.

(4) Whoever maintains, operates, or provides financial means for the operation of a brothel in which a person who has not reached eighteen years of age engages in prostitution is punishable by imprisonment from two years to eight years.

(5) * The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years if the exploitation of child prostitution is committed as a special recidivist.

## 204. § * (1) Anyone who, with regard to a pornographic recording depicting a person who has not reached the age of eighteen,

a) acquires or possesses it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years,

b) offers, transfers or makes it accessible, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years,

c) produces, distributes, trades in it, or makes such recording accessible to the general public, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to ten years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), from five to ten years in the case of point b), and from five to fifteen years in the case of point c), if the offense defined therein is committed

a) against a person who has not reached the age of twelve,

b) against a person under the perpetrator's education, supervision, care or medical treatment, or by abusing another power or influence relationship with respect to the victim, or by exploiting the victim's vulnerable situation,

c) by an official person using this capacity,

d) with regard to a recording containing maltreatment or the application of violence, or

e) as a special recidivist.

(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), from five to fifteen years in the case of point b), and from five to twenty years in the case of point c), if the offense defined therein is committed with regard to a recording containing maltreatment or the application of violence against a person who has not reached the age of twelve.

(4) Anyone who

a) provides material resources for the offense defined in point c) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years,

b) commits preparatory acts for the offense, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(5) Anyone who, with regard to a pornographic recording depicting a person who has reached the age of fourteen but has not reached the age of eighteen,

a) acquires or possesses it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years,

b) produces it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years, if none of the circumstances defined in points b)–e) of paragraph (2) are present.

(6) Anyone who invites a person or persons who have not reached the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic recording shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(7) Anyone who invites a person or persons who have reached the age of fourteen but have not reached the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic recording shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years, if none of the circumstances defined in points b)–e) of paragraph (2) are present.

(8) For the purposes of this Section, pornographic recording means: the depiction of another person or other persons with sexuality in a grossly offensive manner with regard to decency, purposely aimed at arousing sexual desire, including the realistic depiction of a non-existent person or persons.

204/A. § * (1) Anyone who

a) participates in a pornographic performance in which a person or persons who have not reached the age of eighteen appear, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years,

b) features a person or persons who have not reached the age of eighteen in a pornographic performance or organizes such a pornographic performance, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to ten years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), and from five to fifteen years in the case of point b), if the offense defined therein is committed

a) against a person who has not reached the age of twelve,

b) against a person under the perpetrator's education, supervision, care or medical treatment, or by abusing another power or influence relationship with the victim,

c) by an official person using this capacity,

d) with regard to a performance presenting maltreatment or the application of violence, or

e) as a special recidivist.

(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years in the case of point a) of paragraph (1), and from five to twenty years in the case of point b), if the offense defined therein is committed with regard to a performance presenting maltreatment or the application of violence against a person who has not reached the age of twelve.

(4) Anyone who

a) provides material resources for the offense defined in point b) of paragraph (1), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years,

b) commits preparatory acts for the offense, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(5) Anyone who invites a person or persons who have not reached the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic performance shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(6) Anyone who invites a person or persons who have reached the age of fourteen but have not reached the age of eighteen to appear in a pornographic performance shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years, if none of the circumstances defined in points b)–e) of paragraph (2) are present.

(7) For the purposes of this Section, pornographic performance means: an act or performance displaying the sexuality of another person or other persons with grossly offensive openness with regard to decency, purposely aimed at arousing sexual desire.

## 205. §

(1) Anyone who exhibits themselves before another person in an indecent manner for the purpose of arousing or satisfying their sexual desire shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.

(2) A person who has reached the age of eighteen who behaves indecently before a person who has not reached the age of fourteen for the purpose of arousing or satisfying their sexual desire, if no more serious offense is committed, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(3) If no more serious offense is committed, anyone who behaves indecently towards another person in a manner that violates the victim's human dignity shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

## 206. §

Expulsion may also be imposed against a perpetrator of exploitation of child prostitution, facilitation of prostitution, being kept and indecency.

## 207. §

The offense defined in paragraph (1) of Section 196, in point a) of paragraph (1) of Section 197, and in paragraph (3) of Section 205 is punishable only upon private motion, unless an offense not punishable upon private motion is also committed in connection with it.

207/A. § * For the purposes of paragraph (3a) of Section 200, point b) of paragraph (5) of Section 200, paragraph (4) of Section 201 and paragraph (5) of Section 203, offenses of similar nature from the perspective of special recidivism are human trafficking and forced labor, pandering, facilitation of prostitution and exploitation of child prostitution.

## 208. §

(1) A person obliged to educate, supervise or care for a minor – including the cohabitant of a parent or guardian exercising parental supervision, as well as a parent deprived of parental supervisory rights, if they live in a common household or in one dwelling with the minor – who seriously violates their duty arising from this task and thereby endangers the minor's physical, intellectual, moral or emotional development, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) If no more serious offense is committed, a person who has reached the age of eighteen who

a) incites or attempts to incite a person who has not reached the age of eighteen to commit an offense or misdemeanor, or to lead a depraved lifestyle,

b) offers a person who has not reached the age of eighteen for the commission of an offense,

shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

## 209. § Anyone who

a) violates the statutory provisions on the employment of persons who have not reached the age of eighteen, or

b) * employs a third-country national who has not reached the age of eighteen and does not have a work permit issued for the purpose of work, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

## 210. §

(1) Whoever, through their own fault, obstructs the establishment or maintenance of contact between a minor placed with them by administrative decision and a person entitled to maintain contact with the minor, even after the imposition of a fine applied to enforce contact, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) * The perpetrator shall not be punishable if they adequately ensure contact until the issuance of the first instance decision on the merits and commence the replacement of the missed forms of contact.

## 211. §

(1) Whoever takes away a minor placed on the basis of an enforceable administrative decision from the person with whom the authority placed them, without their consent, for the purpose of permanent change of placement, or keeps the minor hidden or in secret, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) Whoever, in the commission of the offense defined in paragraph (1), uses violence at the time of removal, or makes a direct threat against life or bodily integrity, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

## 212. §

(1) Whoever, through their own fault, fails to fulfill their child support obligation prescribed by law and in an enforceable administrative decision, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever, through their own fault, fails to fulfill their maintenance obligation prescribed by law and in an enforceable administrative decision, and thereby subjects the beneficiary to severe deprivation, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) * Pursuant to paragraph (1), the perpetrator shall not be punishable; in the case of paragraph (2), their punishment may be mitigated without limitation if they fulfill their obligation by the issuance of the first instance decision on the merits.

212/A. § * (1) Whoever regularly, against the parent of their child, or against their relative, former spouse, former cohabitant, guardian, ward, legal guardian or ward living in a common household or dwelling at the time of commission or previously,

a) engages in seriously offensive, humiliating and violent conduct against human dignity,

b) withholds material assets belonging to the common economy or common property and thereby subjects the victim to severe deprivation, if a more serious offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever regularly commits against the person defined in paragraph (1)

a) bodily harm according to Section 164 paragraph (2) or defamation according to Section 227 paragraph (2), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years,

b) bodily harm according to Section 164 paragraphs (3) and (4), violation of personal freedom according to Section 194 paragraph (1) or coercion, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.

(3) An exclusion order may also be imposed against the perpetrator of domestic violence.

(4) The offense defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable only upon private motion.

## 213. §

(1) Whoever alters or terminates another person's family status shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the violation of family status is committed

a) by an employee of a healthcare provider or child welfare or child protection institution in the course of their occupation,

b) by a person obliged to educate, supervise or care for a person who has not reached the age of eighteen.

(3) If the violation of family status is committed by negligence by an employee of a healthcare provider or child welfare or child protection institution in the course of their occupation, they shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

## 214. §

Whoever enters into another marriage during the subsistence of their marriage, or whoever enters into marriage with a married person, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

## 215. § Whoever

a) restricts another person in their freedom of conscience by violence or threat,

b) obstructs another person in the free exercise of their religion by violence or threat, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

## 216. §

(1) Whoever, on account of another person's membership or perceived membership in any national, ethnic, racial or religious group or certain groups of the population, in particular their disability, gender identity or sexual orientation, engages in provocatively anti-social conduct capable of causing alarm in a member of that group, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) Whoever, on account of another person's membership or perceived membership in any national, ethnic, racial or religious group or certain groups of the population, in particular their disability, gender identity or sexual orientation, maltreats them or coerces them by violence or threat to do, not do or tolerate something, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.

(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the violence against a member of a community is committed

a) while armed,

b) while having armed oneself,

c) causing significant harm to interests,

d) with the ill-treatment of the victim,

e) in a group, or

f) in a criminal conspiracy.

(4) Whoever commits preparation for violence against a member of a community shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

## 217. §

* (1) Whoever obstructs another person in the exercise of their right to association or assembly by violence or threat shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) Whoever obstructs, makes impossible or otherwise frustrates the holding of an assembly, or seriously disrupts an assembly, if a more serious offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

217/A. § * Whoever resists by violence or threat the organizer, leader or stewards of an assembly in the lawful performance of their stewarding duties at an assembly, or maltreats them while they are performing their stewarding duties, if another offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

217/B. § * (1) Whoever violates the restrictions ensuring the peaceful character of the assembly under the law on the right of assembly, if a more serious offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The leader of the assembly shall be punishable according to paragraph (1) if a steward employed by them violates the restrictions ensuring the peaceful character of the assembly and they do not immediately take measures to remove the steward upon becoming aware of this.

217/C. § * Whoever organizes a prohibited assembly or calls for participation in it under the law on the right of assembly, if another offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

## 218. § (1) Whoever performs, without the consent or authorization of the person entitled, or fails to provide the appropriate information required by law for the exercise of the right to consent and authorization, regarding

a) medical intervention related to the alteration of the human genome, the alteration of the genome of an embryo, human reproduction or the selection of the sex of the child to be born,

b) medical research that can be performed on humans, embryos or germ cells,

c) organ or tissue removal for transplantation purposes, or organ or tissue transplantation, that requires consent or authorization, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) Whoever removes an organ or tissue from a deceased person despite a declaration of objection shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

(3) Whoever, by negligence, performs medical intervention, medical research, organ or tissue removal, or organ or tissue transplantation according to paragraph (1) that requires consent or authorization, without the consent or authorization of the person entitled, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

## 219. §

(1) * Whoever, in violation of provisions set out in statutory law on the protection or processing of personal data or in binding legal acts of the European Union, for the purpose of gaining profit or causing significant harm to interests

a) processes personal data unlawfully or for purposes other than intended, or

b) fails to implement measures serving the security of data, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) * Also punishable according to paragraph (1) is whoever, in violation of provisions set out in statutory law on the protection or processing of personal data or in binding legal acts of the European Union, fails to fulfill their obligation to provide information necessary for the exercise of the data subject's right of access, and thereby significantly violates the interests of another or others.

(3) * The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years if the abuse of personal data is committed with respect to special data or criminal personal data.

(4) The punishment for a felony is imprisonment not exceeding three years if the abuse of personal data is committed as an official person or by using a public mandate.

## 220. § (1) Whoever, in violation of statutory provisions on the publicity of data of public interest

a) conceals data of public interest from the data requester, or fails to fulfill their obligation to provide information after the court has finally ordered the disclosure of the data of public interest,

b) makes data of public interest inaccessible or falsifies it, or

c) makes false or falsified data of public interest accessible or public, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(2) The punishment for a felony is imprisonment not exceeding three years if the abuse of data of public interest is committed for unlawful gain.

## 221. §

(1) Whoever enters or remains in another person's dwelling, other premises, or the enclosed place belonging thereto by force, threat, or simulating official proceedings, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(2) Punishable for a felony with imprisonment not exceeding three years is whoever enters or remains in another person's dwelling, other premises, or the enclosed place belonging thereto, against the will of the person residing there or having control over it, or by deception

a) at night,

b) armed,

c) having armed themselves, or

d) as a group.

(3) Whoever prevents another from entering their dwelling, other premises, or the enclosed place belonging thereto,

a) if committed in the manner specified in paragraph (1), is punishable according to paragraph (1),

b) if committed in the manner specified in paragraph (2), is punishable according to paragraph (2).

(4) The punishment for a felony is imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense specified in paragraph (1) is committed in the manner specified in paragraph (2).

## 222. §

(1) Whoever, for the purpose of intimidating another or arbitrarily interfering with another's private life or daily conduct of life, harasses them regularly or persistently, if a more serious offense is not committed, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) Whoever, for the purpose of causing fear

a) threatens another or their relative with regard to them with the commission of a punishable act of violence against a person or causing public danger, or

b) creates the appearance that an event will occur that harms or directly endangers another's life, physical integrity or health, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(3) * Whoever commits harassment

a) against their spouse, former spouse, cohabitant or former cohabitant,

b) against a person under their education, supervision, care or medical treatment,

c) by abusing their position of power or influence, or

d) against an official person, at a place or time incompatible with their official activity, is punishable in cases specified in paragraph (1) with imprisonment not exceeding two years, and in cases specified in paragraph (2) for a felony with imprisonment not exceeding three years.

(4) * A person who has reached the age of eighteen who commits the harassment specified in paragraph (1) against a person who has not reached the age of eighteen is punishable with imprisonment not exceeding two years.

## 223. §

(1) Whoever discloses without good reason a private secret that came to their knowledge by virtue of their occupation or public mandate is punishable for a misdemeanor with detention.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding one year if the offense causes significant harm to interests.

## 224. § (1) Whoever

a) destroys another person's closed consignment containing a communication, opens it for the purpose of learning its content, obtains it, or hands it over to an unauthorized person for such purpose, or

b) * eavesdrops on a communication transmitted to another via an electronic communications network – including an information system –, if a more serious offense is not committed, is punishable for a misdemeanor with detention.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding one year if the offense specified in paragraph (1) is committed by using one's occupation or public mandate.

(3) The punishment is

a) imprisonment not exceeding two years if the offense specified in paragraph (1),

b) for a felony imprisonment not exceeding three years if the offense specified in paragraph (2) causes significant harm to interests.

## 225. §

(1) Whoever induces another, by exploiting their vulnerable life situation, to behave in a self-degrading manner, if a more serious offense is not committed, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years if the perpetrator

a) gives or promises consideration during the inducement to behave as specified in paragraph (1),

b) makes a recording of the person degrading themselves, or makes such recording accessible to the general public.

(3) The punishment for a felony is imprisonment not exceeding three years if the offense is committed as a business.

## 226. §

(1) Whoever states or spreads a fact suitable for impairing the honor of someone before another, or uses an expression directly referring to such a fact, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years if the defamation is committed

a) for a base motive or purpose,

b) before a large public, or

c) causing significant harm to interests.

(3) * Not punishable for defamation is the act of whoever commits what is specified in paragraph (1) in the context of free discussion of public affairs through a press product or media service, provided that their act is not directed at the manifest and seriously derogatory denial of the victim's human dignity.

226/A. § * (1) Whoever, for the purpose of impairing the honor of another or others, makes a false, falsified or untrue audio or video recording, if another offense is not committed, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding one year.

226/B. § * (1) Whoever, for the purpose of impairing the honor of another or others, makes a false, falsified or untrue audio or video recording accessible, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(2) * The punishment for a felony is imprisonment not exceeding three years if the offense is committed

a) before a large public, or

b) causing significant harm to interests.

## 227. §

(1) Whoever, in addition to what is specified in Section 226, uses an expression suitable for impairing honor against another, or commits another such act

a) in connection with the victim's performance of their work duties, fulfillment of their public mandate or public interest activity, or

b) before a large public, is punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) Punishable according to paragraph (1) is whoever commits the insult by assault.

(3) * Not punishable for insult is the act of whoever commits what is specified in paragraph (1) in the context of free discussion of public affairs through a press product or media service, provided that their act is not directed at the manifest and seriously derogatory denial of the victim's human dignity.

## 228. §

Whoever desecrates a deceased person or their memory in the manner defined in § 226 or § 227 shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with the penalty specified therein.

## 229. §

(1) The perpetrator is not punishable for the offense defined in §§ 226-228 if the fact capable of impairing honor proves to be true.

(2) Proof of truth is admissible if the assertion, dissemination of the fact, or the use of an expression directly referring to it was justified by the public interest or the legitimate interest of anyone.

## 230. § A restraining order may also be imposed against the perpetrator of harassment.

## 231. §

(1) The offense defined in § 218 is punishable only upon private prosecution, except if an offense punishable without private prosecution is also committed in connection therewith. In the case of § 218 paragraph (2), the private prosecution may be initiated by the person entitled to make a statement as defined in the law on healthcare, or by the relative or heir of the deceased.

(2) The offense defined in §§ 221-228 is punishable only upon private prosecution, except if the offense defined in § 227 is committed against a member of a law enforcement agency.

(3) In the case of § 228, the private prosecution may be initiated by the relative or heir of the deceased.

## 232. §

(1) Whoever endangers the life or physical integrity of another or others by damaging or destroying a traffic route, vehicle, operational equipment or their accessories, by creating an obstacle, by removing or altering traffic signs, by giving misleading signals, by using violence or threat against the driver of a moving vehicle, or in another similar manner, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

(2) The punishment is

a) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense causes serious bodily injury,

b) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass casualty,

c) imprisonment from five years to ten years if the offense causes death,

d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years if the offense causes the death of more than two persons or causes a fatal mass casualty.

(3) Whoever commits preparation for the offense defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(4) Whoever commits the offense defined in paragraph (1) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year, and in the cases defined in paragraph (2), according to the distinctions made therein, by imprisonment not exceeding two years, three years, from one year to five years, or from two years to eight years.

(5) The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – in a case deserving special consideration it may be waived – against a person who voluntarily eliminates the danger before any harmful consequence results therefrom.

## 233. §

(1) Whoever endangers the life or physical integrity of another or others by violating the rules of rail, air or water traffic shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

(2) The punishment is

a) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense causes serious bodily injury,

b) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass casualty,

c) imprisonment from five years to ten years if the offense causes death,

d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years if the offense causes the death of more than two persons or causes a fatal mass casualty.

(3) Whoever commits the offense defined in paragraph (1) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year, and in the cases defined in paragraph (2), according to the distinctions made therein, by imprisonment not exceeding two years, three years, from one year to five years, or from two years to eight years.

(4) The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – in a case deserving special consideration it may be waived – against a person who voluntarily eliminates the danger before any harmful consequence results therefrom.

## 234. §

(1) Whoever exposes the life or physical integrity of another or others to immediate danger by violating the rules of road traffic on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

(2) The punishment is

a) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense causes serious bodily injury,

b) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass casualty,

c) imprisonment from five years to ten years if the offense causes death,

d) imprisonment from five years to fifteen years if the offense causes the death of more than two persons or causes a fatal mass casualty.

## 235. §

(1) Whoever by violating the rules of road traffic causes serious bodily injury to another or others by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) The punishment is

a) imprisonment not exceeding three years if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass casualty,

b) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense causes death,

c) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense causes the death of more than two persons or causes a fatal mass casualty.

## 236. §

(1) Whoever operates a rail or air vehicle, a motorized floating facility, or on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic, a motorized vehicle while intoxicated, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(2) The punishment for a felony is

a) imprisonment not exceeding three years if the offense causes serious bodily injury,

b) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass casualty,

c) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense causes death,

d) imprisonment from five years to ten years if the offense causes the death of more than two persons or causes a fatal mass casualty.

(3) Whoever while intoxicated causes the consequence defined in paragraph (2) by operating a non-motorized floating facility or on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic, a non-motorized vehicle, shall be punishable according to the distinctions made therein.

## 237. §

(1) Whoever operates a rail or air vehicle, a motorized floating facility, or on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic, a motorized vehicle under the influence of a substance adversely affecting driving ability, other than alcohol resulting from the consumption of alcoholic beverages, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(2) The punishment for a felony is

a) imprisonment not exceeding three years if the offense causes serious bodily injury,

b) imprisonment from one year to five years if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment of health or mass casualty,

c) imprisonment from two years to eight years if the offense causes death,

d) imprisonment from five years to ten years if the offense causes the death of more than two persons or causes a fatal mass casualty.

(3) Whoever under the influence of a substance adversely affecting driving ability, other than alcohol resulting from the consumption of alcoholic beverages, causes the consequence defined in paragraph (2) by operating a non-motorized floating facility, or on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic, a non-motorized vehicle, shall be punishable according to the distinctions made therein.

## 238. §

(1) Whoever allows a person in an intoxicated or drugged state, or a person otherwise unfit to drive, to drive a railway or air vehicle, mechanically propelled watercraft, or mechanically propelled vehicle on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) The punishment for a felony is

a) imprisonment not exceeding three years, if the offense causes permanent disability, serious impairment to health, or mass casualty,

b) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the offense causes death,

c) imprisonment from two years to eight years, if the offense causes the death of more than two persons, or causes a mass casualty with fatalities.

## 239. §

If the driver of a vehicle involved in a traffic accident does not stop at the scene or leaves it before ascertaining whether anyone was injured or requires assistance due to danger directly threatening their life or bodily integrity, if a more serious crime is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

239/A. § * (1) Whoever drives on a public road or on a private road not closed to public traffic a road vehicle defined in the law on passenger transport services or a vehicle providing special passenger transport services that has been modified without permission or in a manner different from the permit, if a more serious crime is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment not exceeding two years if the offense is committed with a school bus or a road vehicle used for transporting persons with special needs – such as persons with reduced mobility, elderly or sick persons.

(3) Whoever modifies or has modified without permission the vehicle defined in this Section, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by confinement.

239/B. § * (1) Whoever drives a vehicle falling within the scope of a driving ban while under the effect of such ban, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year.

(2) Whoever commits the offense while under the effect of a driving ban imposed in criminal proceedings, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

## 240. §

(1) The provisions established for offenses committed on public roads shall also apply when the violation of road traffic rules causes at least serious bodily injury or death not on a public road.

(2) For the application of Sections 233–235, provisions relating to pedestrians and passengers shall not be considered traffic rules.

(3) * For the application of Sections 236 and 238, a person in an intoxicated state is one whose body contains alcohol from the consumption of alcoholic beverages capable of producing a value greater than 0.50 grams/liter blood alcohol concentration or 0.25 milligrams/liter breath alcohol concentration.

## 241. §

(1) Whoever, by significant pollution or otherwise,

a) endangers,

b) damages to such an extent that its natural or previous state can only be restored by intervention,

c) damages to such an extent that its natural or previous state cannot be restored,

the land, the air, the water, the living world, and their components, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years in the case specified in point a), from one year to five years in the case specified in point b), from two years to eight years in the case specified in point c).

(2) Whoever commits environmental damage by negligence, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding one year in the case of paragraph (1) point a), two years in the case of point b), three years in the case of point c).

(3) * In the cases specified in paragraph (1) point a) and in the first and second sentences of paragraph (2), the offender shall not be punishable, and in the case of paragraph (1) point b) their punishment may be reduced without limit, if by the time of the first-instance decision on the merits they eliminate the danger or environmental damage caused by the offense and restore the original state of the damaged environment.

(4) For the application of this Section, pollution means: the loading of the land, the air, the water, the living world, and their components exceeding the emission limit values established by law or by authority decision.

## 242. § (1) Whoever

a) unlawfully acquires, keeps, places on the market, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, transports through it, trades in, damages or destroys a specimen of a strictly protected living organism,

b) specimens of a protected living organism or of a plant or animal species of conservation significance in the European Union, provided that their combined monetary value determined by separate legislation reaches the lowest monetary value established for specimens of strictly protected living organisms,

c) a specimen of a living organism falling under the scope of Annexes A and B of the EC Council Regulation on the protection afforded by regulating trade in wild fauna and flora species,

shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the damage to nature causes destruction of specimens of living organisms to such an extent

a) that in the case of paragraph (1) points a) or b) the combined monetary value determined by separate legislation of the destroyed specimens of living organisms reaches twice the highest monetary value established for specimens of strictly protected living organisms,

b) which in the case of paragraph (1) point c) endangers the survival of the population of the living organism.

(2a) * Punishable according to paragraph (2) is whoever commits damage to nature by using poison or by placing bait suitable for destroying the animal, endangering the life of multiple animals.

(3) Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (2) by negligence, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(3a) * Whoever commits preparation for the offense specified in paragraph (2) or (2a), shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(4) For the application of this Section, specimen of a living organism means:

- a) all developmental stages, forms, states of the specimen of the living organism,
- b) a specimen created as a cross and hybrid of living organisms,
- c) a derivative of a specimen of the living organism, which shall be understood as the dead organism, as well as any part thereof or of the specimen of the living organism, and also any product or preparation made from any of the foregoing or containing a component derived from any of them.

## 243. §

(1) Whoever unlawfully significantly alters a Natura 2000 area, a protected cave, a protected natural area or the life community of protected living organisms or their habitat, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment not exceeding three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if the damage to nature causes significant damage or destruction of the Natura 2000 area, the protected cave, the protected natural area or the life community of protected living organisms or their habitat.

(3) Whoever commits the offense specified in paragraph (2) by negligence, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment not exceeding two years.

(4) For the application of this Section, Natura 2000 area shall be understood as the concept defined in the law on the protection of nature.

## 244. § (1) Aki

a) gerinces állatot indokolatlanul oly módon bántalmaz, vagy gerinces állattal szemben indokolatlanul olyan bánásmódot alkalmaz, amely alkalmas arra, hogy annak maradandó egészségkárosodását vagy pusztulását okozza,

b) gerinces állatát vagy veszélyes állatát elűzi, elhagyja vagy kiteszi, vétség miatt két évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) * A büntetés bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha az állatkínzást

a) az állat különös szenvedését okozva,

b) több állat maradandó egészségkárosodását vagy pusztulását okozva,

c) nagy nyilvánosság előtt,

d) az állatok védelméről és kíméletéről szóló törvényben meghatározott, az állat kedvtelésből való tartásától, illetve az érintett állatfaj tartásától eltiltással érintett állattal szemben az eltiltás tartama alatt, vagy

e) állatkínzás vagy tiltott állatviadal szervezése bűncselekmény miatti elítélést követő két éven belül követik el.

(3) * A büntetés egy évtől öt évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha az állatkínzást

a) méreg alkalmazásával vagy az állat elpusztítására alkalmas csalétek kihelyezésével több állat pusztulását okozva, vagy

b) különös visszaesőként követik el.

(4) * A (3) bekezdés szerint büntetendő, aki az (1) bekezdés a) pontjában foglalt bűncselekményt szaporítással összefüggő haszonszerzési cél érdekében, tíznél nagyobb számú kedvtelésből tartott állatra, a jó gazda gondosságának elmulasztásával követi el.

(5) * Aki a (3) bekezdés a) pontjában meghatározott bűncselekményre irányuló előkészületet követ el, vétség miatt két évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(6) * A (3) bekezdés b) pontja alkalmazásában a különös visszaesés szempontjából hasonló jellegű bűncselekmény az állat pusztulását okozva elkövetett természetkárosítás, az állatkínzás, a tiltott állatviadal szervezése.

## 245. § Aki

a) vadászterületen vadászatra való jogosultság nélkül, illetve idegen vadászterületen vadászként engedély nélkül vad elejtésére vagy elfogására irányuló tevékenységet végez,

b) külön jogszabályban meghatározott, a vadfaj valamennyi egyedére kiterjedő vadászati tilalmi idő hatálya alá eső vadfaj egyedét ejti vagy fogja el,

c) külön jogszabályban meghatározott tiltott vadászati eszközzel, tiltott vadászati módon vagy kíméleti területen vad, illetve fokozottan védett vagy védett gerinces állat elejtésére vagy elfogására irányuló tevékenységet végez, bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

## 246. § Aki

a) jogosulatlanul halászhálóval vagy más halászati eszközzel – a horgászatot kivéve – halfogásra irányuló tevékenységet végez,

b) külön jogszabályban meghatározott tiltott eszközzel, tiltott módon vagy kíméleti területen halfogásra irányuló tevékenységet végez, vétség miatt két évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

## 247. §

(1) Aki gerinces állat részvételével állatviadalt szervez, tart, ilyen állatviadalra fogadást szervez vagy fogadást köt, bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(1a) * Aki állatviadalon részt vesz, vétség miatt két évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) Aki állatviadal céljára gerinces állatot megszerez, tart, tenyészt, kiképez, idomít, vagy forgalmaz, vétség miatt két évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(3) * Ha a tiltott állatviadal szervezését különös visszaesőként követik el, a büntetés

a) az (1) bekezdésben meghatározott esetben egy évtől öt évig terjedő szabadságvesztés,

b) az (1a) és a (2) bekezdésben meghatározott esetben három évig terjedő szabadságvesztés.

(4) * A (3) bekezdés alkalmazásában a különös visszaesés szempontjából hasonló jellegű bűncselekmény az állat pusztulását okozva elkövetett természetkárosítás, az állatkínzás, a tiltott állatviadal szervezése, a veszélyes állat, illetve veszélyes eb tartásával kapcsolatos kötelezettség megszegése.

## 248. § * (1) Aki

a) nyilvántartásba vétel vagy bejelentés nélkül, illetve engedély nélkül vagy az engedély kereteit túllépve végez hulladékgazdálkodási tevékenységet, vagy

b) hulladékkal más jogellenes tevékenységet végez és az alkalmas az emberi élet, testi épség, egészség, a föld, a víz, a levegő vagy azok összetevői, illetve élő szervezet egyedének veszélyeztetésére bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) Aki arra a célra hatóság által nem engedélyezett helyen

a) az emberi élet, testi épség, egészség, a föld, a víz, a levegő vagy azok összetevői, illetve élő szervezet egyedének veszélyeztetésére alkalmas vagy

b) jelentős mennyiségű hulladékot elhelyez, bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(3) A büntetés egy évtől öt évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha az (1) vagy a (2) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekményt

a) veszélyes hulladékra,

b) különösen jelentős mennyiségű hulladékra, vagy

c) különös visszaesőként követik el.

(4) A büntetés két évtől nyolc évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha a (3) bekezdés a) pontjában meghatározott bűncselekmény a (3) bekezdés b) pontja szerint is minősül.

(5) Aki a bűncselekményt gondatlanságból követi el, vétség miatt

a) az (1) és (2) bekezdésben meghatározott esetben egy évig,

b) a (3) bekezdésben meghatározott esetben két évig,

c) a (4) bekezdésben meghatározott esetben három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(6) E § alkalmazásában

a) különös visszaesés szempontjából hasonló jellegű bűncselekmény a környezetkárosítás és a természetkárosítás;

b) hulladékgazdálkodási tevékenység a hulladékról szóló törvény szerinti hulladékgazdálkodás azzal, hogy a szállítás fogalmába bele kell érteni az országba történő behozatalt, onnan történő kivitelt, valamint az azon történő átszállítást;

c) jelentős mennyiségű hulladék az 1000 kg-ot vagy a 10 köbmétert meghaladó mennyiségű hulladék;

d) különösen jelentős mennyiségű hulladék a jelentős mennyiségű hulladék tízszerese.

## 249. §

(1) Aki ózonréteget lebontó anyagot vagy ilyen anyagot tartalmazó terméket gyárt, felhasznál, az ország területére behoz, onnan kivisz vagy forgalomba hoz, bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) Aki az ózonréteget lebontó anyaggal visszaélést gondatlanságból követi el, vétség miatt egy évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

## 250. § (1) Aki veszélyes radioaktív anyagot engedély vagy bejelentés nélkül, illetve az engedély kereteit túllépve

a) előállít, tárol, elhelyez, szállít,

b) megszerez, birtokol, kezel, forgalomba hoz, feldolgoz vagy más módon felhasznál, tartásra nem jogosult személynek átad, ártalmatlanít, az ország területére behoz, onnan kivisz, vagy azon átszállít, bűntett miatt egy évtől öt évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) Aki a bűncselekményt bűnszövetségben követi el, két évtől nyolc évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(3) Aki radioaktív anyaggal való visszaélésre irányuló előkészületet követ el, bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(4) * Aki az (1) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekményt gondatlanságból követi el, vétség miatt két évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(5) E § alkalmazásában veszélyes radioaktív anyag: a természetben előforduló vagy mesterségesen előállított anyag, amelynek egy vagy több összetevője ionizáló sugárzást bocsát ki, és amely az emberi életre és egészségre, vagy az élő és élettelen környezetre veszélyes.

## 251. §

(1) Aki engedély nélkül vagy az engedély kereteit túllépve nukleáris létesítményt üzemeltet, bűntett miatt egy évtől öt évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) Aki az (1) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekményt bűnszövetségben követi el, két évtől nyolc évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(3) Aki nukleáris létesítmény üzemeltetésével való visszaélésre irányuló előkészületet követ el, három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

## 252. §

(1) Anyone who deceives the body or person authorized to make decisions in order to obtain a license necessary for the application of nuclear energy shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) Anyone who fails to fulfill their obligation to report in connection with the application of nuclear energy shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.

## 253. §

The perpetrator of environmental damage, damage to nature, poaching, illegal fishing, violation of the waste management order, and organizing prohibited animal fighting may also be subject to expulsion.

253/A. § * For the purposes of this chapter, poison is a dangerous substance or mixture which, based on its toxicological properties, when inhaled, swallowed or absorbed through the skin, endangers the life of an animal or may cause particular suffering or permanent damage to the health of the animal.

## 254. §

(1) Anyone who commits an act directly aimed at changing the constitutional order of Hungary by force or threat of force shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment.

(2) Anyone who commits preparation for the violent change of the constitutional order shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years.

(3) Anyone whose voluntary withdrawal results in the discontinuation of the offense, or who voluntarily prevents its continuation, shall not be punishable for the violent change of the constitutional order.

## 255. §

(1) Anyone who establishes or leads an organization whose purpose is to change the constitutional order of Hungary by force or threat of force shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from five to ten years.

(2) Anyone who participates in the organization specified in paragraph (1) shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) Anyone whose voluntary withdrawal results in the discontinuation of the conspiracy, or who voluntarily prevents its continuation, shall not be punishable for conspiracy against the constitutional order.

## 256. § (1) Anyone who participates in a riot whose direct purpose is to

a) the National Assembly,

b) the President of the Republic,

c) * the Curia,

d) * the Government, or

e) * the Constitutional Court prevent from exercising the powers defined in the Fundamental Law by force or threat of force, or compel to take action, shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) Anyone who organizes or leads the riot specified in paragraph (1) shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(3) Anyone who commits preparation for sedition shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years.

(4) A participant who leaves the riot voluntarily or at the call of the authorities before the commencement of violent acts shall not be punishable for sedition.

## 257. §

(1) Anyone who, for the purpose of disrupting the constitutional order of Hungary, destroys, renders unusable or damages a public utility, production, public traffic or telecommunications facility or its equipment, public building or structure, product stock, war materiel or other property of similar importance by reason of its purpose, shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment if the sabotage results in particularly serious harm.

(3) Anyone who commits preparation for sabotage shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.

## 258. §

(1) A Hungarian citizen who establishes or maintains contact with a foreign government or foreign organization for the purpose of violating Hungary's independence, territorial integrity or constitutional order shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment if the treason is committed

a) causing serious harm,

b) by exploiting state service or official mandate,

c) in time of war, or

d) by calling in or using foreign armed forces.

(3) Anyone who commits preparation for treason shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years, or from two to eight years in time of war.

## 259. §

A Hungarian citizen who, by abusing their state service or official mandate, establishes or maintains contact with a foreign government or foreign organization, and thereby endangers Hungary's independence, territorial integrity or constitutional order, shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from two to eight years, or from five to fifteen years in time of war.

## 260. §

(1) Anyone who, in time of war, for the purpose of weakening Hungary's military strength, enters into communication with the enemy, provides assistance to them, or causes harm to their own or allied armed forces, shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment.

(2) Anyone who commits preparation for supporting the enemy shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.

## 261. §

(1) Anyone who conducts espionage activities against Hungary on behalf of a foreign power or foreign organization shall be punished for a felony with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) Anyone who commits the espionage specified in paragraph (1) by disclosing strictly classified information shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(3) Anyone who commits preparation for espionage shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years.

(4) Anyone who – before engaging in any other espionage activity – reports their offer or undertaking to engage in espionage activities to the authorities or the competent body of the state, and fully discloses their foreign connection, shall not be punishable for offering or undertaking espionage activities.

261/A. § * Anyone who conducts espionage activities on behalf of a third state outside the European Union against the European Parliament, the European Commission or the Council of the European Union shall be punishable in accordance with Section 261.

## 262. §

Anyone who commits espionage on the territory of a state allied with Hungary, or against allied armed forces, in accordance with an international treaty in force containing an obligation of mutual military assistance, shall be punishable in accordance with Section 261.

## 263. §

(1) * Anyone who obtains credible knowledge that violent change of the constitutional order, conspiracy against the constitutional order, sedition, sabotage, treason, disloyalty, supporting the enemy, espionage, espionage committed against allied armed forces, or espionage against the institutions of the European Union is being prepared, or that such an offense as yet undetected has been committed, and does not report this to the authorities or the competent body of the state as soon as they can, shall be punished for a misdemeanor with imprisonment up to two years.

(2) A relative of the perpetrator shall not be punishable for failure to report a crime against the state.

## 264. §

In the case of the offenses specified in this chapter, the perpetrator may also be subject to expulsion.

## 265. § (1) Whoever with classified data

a) unlawfully obtains or uses,

b) makes accessible to an unauthorized person, or makes inaccessible to an authorized person, commits abuse of classified data.

(2) The punishment is

a) for a misdemeanor detention, if restricted distribution,

b) imprisonment for up to one year, if confidential,

c) for a felony imprisonment for up to three years, if secret,

d) imprisonment from one year to five years, if the crime is committed against strictly secret classified data.

(3) A person authorized by law to use classified data who commits abuse of classified data against restricted distribution, confidential, secret or strictly secret classified data shall be punishable, according to the distinction specified in paragraph (2), by imprisonment for up to one year, up to two years, from one year to five years, or from two years to eight years.

(4) Whoever commits preparation for abuse of classified data specified in paragraph (2) points c)–d) shall be punishable, according to the distinction made therein, for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years, or for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(5) * A person authorized by law to use classified data who commits preparation for abuse of classified data specified in paragraph (2) points c)–d) shall be punishable, according to the distinction made therein, for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years, or from one year to five years.

(6) A person authorized by law to use classified data who commits the crime by negligence shall be punishable, according to the distinction specified in paragraph (2), for a misdemeanor by detention, imprisonment for up to one year, up to two years, or up to three years.

## 266. §

(1) Criminal law protection also extends for thirty days from the initiation of classification to data for which classification has been initiated but the classification procedure has not yet been completed at the time of the commission of the crime, and the perpetrator is aware of this.

## 267. § * (1) Whoever

a) makes data managed in a state register belonging to the national data assets inaccessible to the data controller, or

b) obstructs the operation of a state register belonging to the national data assets without authorization or in violation of the limits of their authorization, if a more serious crime is not committed, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if

a) the crime causes significant harm to interests, or

b) the crime is committed for gain.
