(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if as a result of the crime all data managed in a state register belonging to the national data assets become permanently inaccessible to the data controller.

## 268. § (1) Whoever

a) falsely accuses another person before an authority of committing a crime,

b) brings fabricated evidence of a crime to the attention of an authority against another person, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years if criminal proceedings are initiated against the person concerned on the basis of the false accusation.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years if

a) the accused is convicted on the basis of the false accusation,

b) the false accusation concerns a crime for which the law also threatens the perpetrator with life imprisonment.

(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five years to ten years if the accused is convicted on the basis of a false accusation concerning a crime punishable by life imprisonment.

(5) Whoever falsely accuses another person before an authority of committing a crime because by negligence they are not aware that their statement of fact is false or the evidence is false shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

## 269. § Whoever

a) falsely accuses another person before an authority of a petty offense or a rule violation subject to administrative fine,

b) falsely accuses another person before an authority or the exerciser of disciplinary authority of a disciplinary offense,

c) brings fabricated evidence concerning a petty offense, a rule violation subject to administrative fine or a disciplinary offense to the attention of an authority or the exerciser of disciplinary authority against another person, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

## 270. §

(1) If proceedings have been initiated as a result of a false accusation, until the conclusion of this underlying case, criminal proceedings for false accusation may only be initiated on the basis of a report by the authority conducting the underlying case. Except in the case of such a report, the limitation period for the punishability of false accusation begins on the day the underlying case is concluded.

(2) The punishment of the perpetrator of a false accusation may be mitigated without limit, and in cases deserving special consideration may be omitted, if they disclose the falsity of the accusation to the authority conducting the proceedings before the conclusion of the underlying case.

## 271. §

(1) Whoever makes a report to an authority serving as the basis for criminal proceedings which they know to be false – and the case of Section 268 does not exist – shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever makes a report to an authority or to the exerciser of disciplinary authority serving as the basis for petty offense or disciplinary proceedings which they know to be false – and the case of Section 269 does not exist – shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to one year.

(3) Whoever makes a report to an authority serving as the basis for other administrative proceedings which they know to be false – and the case of Section 269 does not exist – shall be punishable by detention.

## 272. §

(1) A witness who makes a false statement before an authority concerning an essential circumstance of the case, or conceals the truth, commits false testimony.

(2) The provisions on false testimony shall apply to anyone who

a) as an expert gives a false expert opinion or as an expert advisor gives false information,

b) as an interpreter or translator translates falsely,

c) other than in the case of Section 268 paragraph (1) point b) provides a false document or false material evidence in a criminal or civil case.

(3) * The accused in a criminal case cannot be punished under paragraph (2) point c).

(4) * Whoever commits false testimony in a criminal case shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one year to five years. If the false testimony concerns a crime that is also punishable by life imprisonment, the punishment is imprisonment from two years to eight years.

(5) Whoever commits false testimony in a civil case shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years. If the subject of the civil case is a particularly large property value or a particularly significant other interest, the punishment is imprisonment from one year to five years.

(6) Whoever commits false testimony by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

## 273. §

Whoever commits false testimony in petty offense or other administrative proceedings, or in disciplinary proceedings, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

## 274. §

For false testimony, as long as the underlying case in which the false testimony was committed has not been concluded, criminal proceedings may only be initiated on the basis of a report by the authority conducting the underlying case. Except in the case of such a report, the limitation period for the punishability of false testimony begins on the day the underlying case is concluded.

## 275. § (1) Shall not be punishable for false testimony,

a) anyone who would accuse themselves or their relative of committing a crime if the truth were revealed,

b) anyone who may refuse to testify for other reasons but was not warned of this before their interrogation, or

c) anyone whose interrogation is excluded by law.

(2) * The punishment may be mitigated without limit, and in cases deserving special consideration may be omitted, against anyone who reports to the authority conducting the proceedings the falsity of the evidence provided by them before the final or definitive conclusion of the underlying case.

## 276. §

(1) * Whoever attempts to induce another to commit false testimony is punishable in the case of commission in criminal proceedings as a felony by imprisonment for up to three years, in the case of commission in civil proceedings as a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever commits the offense defined in subsection (1) in proceedings pending before a misdemeanor or other authority or before an exerciser of disciplinary jurisdiction is punishable as a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

## 277. §

* A witness who unlawfully refuses to testify before the court in criminal proceedings after the witness admonition is punishable as a misdemeanor by confinement.

## 278. §

(1) Whoever by force or threat compels another not to exercise their lawful rights or not to fulfill their obligations in official proceedings commits the offense of coercion in official proceedings.

(2) * Whoever commits the offense in criminal proceedings is punishable as a felony by imprisonment from one to five years. If the offense is committed in criminal proceedings conducted for an offense also punishable by life imprisonment, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) Whoever commits the offense in civil proceedings is punishable by imprisonment for up to three years. If the subject of the civil case is a particularly high pecuniary value or particularly significant other interest, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years.

(4) If the offense is committed in proceedings pending before a misdemeanor or other authority or before an exerciser of disciplinary jurisdiction, the punishment is imprisonment for up to one year as a misdemeanor.

## 279. §

(1) * Whoever engages in conduct provocatively antisocial or violent that disrupts, obstructs or frustrates the proceedings of an authority, if a more serious offense is not committed, is punishable as a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) * The punishment is imprisonment for up to three years as a felony if the offense defined in subsection (1) is committed in court proceedings.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the offense defined in subsection (1) is committed in a group, armed or in arms.

## 280. §

(1) Whoever discloses a fact, data or circumstance that came to their knowledge as an official witness without the release of the authorized person is punishable as a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) If a more serious offense is not committed, the person who discloses what was said at a closed hearing of the court without the permission of the authorized person is punishable pursuant to subsection (1). *

(3) The person who discloses a fact or data not to be made public that was said at a judicial council meeting or contained in the minutes of the council meeting or its annexes is punishable pursuant to subsection (1).

## 281. §

(1) Whoever fails to communicate to the person subject to criminal proceedings, their defense counsel or the authority a fact upon which the termination of proceedings against the person subject to criminal proceedings or the acquittal of the person subject to proceedings may depend is punishable as a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) If the concealment of an exonerating circumstance relates to an offense also punishable by life imprisonment, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) Whoever fails to communicate to the person subject to misdemeanor, administrative fine or disciplinary proceedings, their legal representative or the authority a fact upon which the termination of proceedings against the person subject to proceedings may depend is punishable as a misdemeanor by confinement.

(4) The following are not punishable for concealment of an exonerating circumstance:

a) a person who by communicating the fact would accuse themselves or their relative of committing aa) an offense in criminal proceedings or official proceedings, ab) an offense or misdemeanor in misdemeanor proceedings, ac) an offense, misdemeanor, violation punishable by administrative fine or disciplinary offense in disciplinary proceedings,

b) a person whose interrogation as a witness is excluded by law.

## 282. §

(1) Whoever, without having agreed with the perpetrator of the offense before its commission,

a) provides assistance for the perpetrator to escape from the pursuit of the authority,

b) attempts to frustrate the success of criminal proceedings, or

c) participates in securing the benefit derived from the offense, is punishable as a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment for up to three years as a felony if the harboring of a criminal is committed for gain.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the harboring of a criminal

a) is committed in connection with an offense defined in Chapter XIII, XIV or XXIV [except failure to report an offense against the state [Section 263, subsection (1)]],

b) * is committed in connection with homicide [Section 160, subsections (1)–(3) and (5)], kidnapping [Section 190, subsections (1)–(4)], human trafficking and forced labor [Section 192, subsections (1)–(6)], terrorist act [Section 314, subsections (1)–(2)], financing of terrorism [Sections 318 and 318/A], seizure of a vehicle [Section 320, subsections (1)–(2)],

c) is committed in connection with an offense not listed in points a)–b) also punishable by life imprisonment, or

d) is committed by an official, a foreign official person during official proceedings, in breach of official duty or by a person performing a public function during proceedings.

(4) Except for subsection (2) and point d) of subsection (3), a person who commits the harboring of a criminal defined in point a) of subsection (1) in the interest of their relative is not punishable.

## 283. §

(1) Whoever escapes from the custody of the authority during criminal proceedings or during the execution of imprisonment or confinement is punishable as a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) * Whoever, for the purpose of evading criminal proceedings conducted against them, leaves during the period of criminal supervision the area, dwelling, other premises, institution, or fenced place belonging thereto designated for them, in violation of the rules of the coercive measure, is punishable as a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2a) * The person who, for the purpose of evading extradition or surrender proceedings conducted against them, leaves during the period of extradition, temporary extradition, temporary surrender, or temporary enforcement criminal supervision the area, dwelling, other premises, institution, or fenced place belonging thereto designated for them, in violation of the rules of the coercive measure, is punishable pursuant to subsection (2).

(3) The punishment is imprisonment for up to one year if the convicted person during the execution of imprisonment does not return upon the expiration of the authorized interruption of sentence, leave, short-term leave or absence for the purpose of evading the execution of the sentence.

(3a) * The punishment is imprisonment for up to one year if the convicted person during the period of reintegration custody leaves the dwelling designated for them and the fenced place belonging thereto or the place of stay according to the schedule designated for them, in violation of the rules of reintegration custody, for the purpose of evading the execution of imprisonment.

(3b) * If the person subject to proceedings escapes from the custody of the authority during the execution of misdemeanor custody or misdemeanor confinement, they are punishable as a misdemeanor by confinement.

(4) The punishment of the perpetrator of escape from custody may be mitigated without limitation if they voluntarily surrender before their place of stay becomes known to the authority.

## 284. §

(1) A prisoner who, together with other prisoners, participates in open resistance seriously endangering the order of detention is punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) Punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years for prisoner mutiny is

a) the initiator, organizer or leader,

b) a participant who uses violence against a person acting against the prisoner mutiny.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years if the prisoner mutiny leads to particularly serious consequences.

(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment if the prisoner mutiny causes death.

(5) Whoever commits a preparatory act for prisoner mutiny is punishable by imprisonment up to three years.

(6) In the case specified in paragraph (1), the punishment may be mitigated without limitation for a person who ceases the resistance voluntarily or upon request of the authority.

## 285. §

(1) An attorney who violates a professional obligation in order to cause unlawful disadvantage to their client is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the attorney abuse is committed for profit.

(3) For the purposes of this section, attorney also means a trainee attorney and any other person who is entitled to legal representation by virtue of their occupation.

## 286. §

(1) * Whoever without authorization and commercially performs attorney, patent agent or notary activities is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.

(2) * The punishment is imprisonment up to three years for a felony if the unauthorized practice is committed by simulating entitlement to attorney, patent agent or notary activities.

## 287. § (1) Whoever

a) removes or violates the seal applied during seizure, attachment or sequestration ordered in official proceedings,

b) opens a sealed room serving for the storage of seized, attached or sequestered property,

c) * makes data subject to data preservation obligation accessible to an unauthorized person, or withdraws or modifies it from the proceedings,

d) * makes electronic data temporarily made inaccessible during criminal proceedings accessible to an unauthorized person, or withdraws or modifies it from the proceedings, is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.

(2) Whoever withdraws property seized, attached or sequestered during official proceedings from enforcement is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(3) * The perpetrator cannot be punished for breaking seals specified in paragraph (2) if before the indictment they return the property – without damage to its substance – to the proceeding authority.

## 288. §

(1) Whoever, during judicial enforcement, after the court has imposed a final fine on them, continues the conduct that gave rise to the fine, or still does not fulfill their obligation arising from enforcement as specified in legislation – not including the obligation specified in the enforceable instrument – is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.

(2) * The perpetrator cannot be punished if before the indictment they fulfill their obligation arising from enforcement as specified in legislation.

## 289. §

Punishable according to §§ 268–282 is whoever commits the offense specified therein during or in connection with proceedings before an international criminal court established by an international treaty promulgated by law or by a binding decision of the United Nations Security Council, or before the Court of Justice of the European Union.

## 290. §

(1) Whoever gives or promises an unlawful advantage to a person performing activities for or on behalf of a business organization, or to another person in consideration of them, in order for them to violate their obligation, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the offense specified in paragraph (1) is committed in connection with a person authorized to take independent measures who performs activities for or on behalf of a business organization.

(3) The punishment is

a) in the case of paragraph (1), imprisonment from one to five years,

b) in the case of paragraph (2), imprisonment from two to eight years, if the bribery is committed in a criminal association or commercially.

(4) Punishable according to paragraphs (1)–(3) is whoever commits the bribery in connection with a person performing activities for or on behalf of a foreign business organization.

(5) * The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – or in cases deserving special consideration may be waived – for the perpetrator of the offense specified in paragraph (1) if they report the offense to the authority before it comes to the authority's knowledge and disclose the circumstances of commission. This provision is not applicable if the offense is committed in a criminal organization.

(6) * Whoever, in connection with the provision of healthcare services, gives or promises an unlawful advantage to a healthcare worker, a person working in healthcare, or to another person in consideration of them, as specified in the law on healthcare, if a more serious offense is not committed, is punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.

## 291. §

(1) * Whoever, in connection with their activity performed for or on behalf of a business organization, requests an unlawful advantage, or accepts the unlawful advantage or its promise, or agrees with the requester or acceptor of an unlawful advantage requested by or given or promised to a third person in consideration of them, is punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) If the perpetrator

a) violates their obligation for the unlawful advantage, is punishable by imprisonment from one to five years,

b) commits the offense specified in paragraph (1) in a criminal association or commercially, is punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) If the perpetrator is a person authorized to take independent measures who performs activities for or on behalf of a business organization, the punishment is

a) in the case specified in paragraph (1), imprisonment from one to five years,

b) in the case specified in paragraph (2) point a), imprisonment from two to eight years,

c) in the case specified in paragraph (2) point b), imprisonment from five to ten years.

(4) Punishable according to paragraphs (1)–(3) is a person performing activities for or on behalf of a foreign business organization who commits the offense specified therein.

(5) * The punishment may be mitigated without limitation – or in cases deserving special consideration may be waived – for the perpetrator of the offense specified in paragraph (1) and paragraph (3) point a) if they report the offense to the authority before it comes to the authority's knowledge, hand over the received unlawful material advantage or its equivalent value to the authority, and disclose the circumstances of commission. This provision is not applicable if the offense is committed in a criminal organization.

(6) * For the purposes of this section, in connection with healthcare services, unlawful advantage means unlawful advantage as specified in the law on healthcare.


This excerpt sits under: A jogszabály mai napon ( 2024.02.14. ) hatályos állapota.

## 292. § *

## 293. §

(1) * Whoever seeks to influence an official person in connection with their functioning through an undue advantage given or promised to them or to another person with regard to them shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.

(2) * The briber shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years if they give or promise the undue advantage in order for the official person to breach their official duty, exceed their authority, or otherwise abuse their official position.

(3) * Whoever commits the criminal offence defined therein in connection with the functioning of a foreign official person shall be punishable pursuant to paragraphs (1) and (2).

(4) The manager of a business organization, or a person authorized to control or supervise and performing activities for or in the interest of the business organization, shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1) if a person performing activities for or in the interest of the business organization commits the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1)-(3) in the interest of the business organization, and the performance of their supervisory or control obligation could have prevented the commission of the criminal offence.

(5) The manager of a business organization, or a person authorized to control or supervise and performing activities for or in the interest of the business organization, shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor if they commit the criminal offence defined in paragraph (4) through negligence.

(6) The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and may be waived in cases deserving special consideration – with respect to the perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1) and (2) if they report the criminal offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and disclose the circumstances of the commission.

## 294. §

(1) * The official person who, in connection with their functioning, requests an undue advantage, accepts the undue advantage or its promise, or agrees with the requester or acceptor of an undue advantage requested by or given or promised to a third person with regard to them, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years for a felony.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two years to eight years if the criminal offence is committed by an official person in a leading position.

(3) In the case defined in paragraph (1), imprisonment from two years to eight years, and in the case defined in paragraph (2), imprisonment from five years to ten years shall be imposed on whoever

a) * for the undue advantage aa) breaches their official duty, ab) exceeds their authority, or ac) otherwise abuses their official position, or

b) commits the criminal offence in a criminal conspiracy or in a professional manner.

(4) A foreign official person who commits the criminal offence defined therein shall be punishable pursuant to paragraphs (1)-(3).

(5) * The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and may be waived in cases deserving special consideration – with respect to the perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1) and (2) if they report the criminal offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority, hand over the received undue pecuniary advantage or its equivalent value to the authority, and disclose the circumstances of the commission. This provision shall not be applicable if the criminal offence is committed in a criminal organization.

## 295. §

(1) * Whoever gives or promises an undue advantage to another person or to someone else with regard to them in order for that person not to exercise their legal rights or not to fulfill their obligations in judicial, arbitration or official proceedings shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.

(2) Whoever commits the criminal offence defined therein in the course of or in connection with proceedings pending before an international criminal court established by an international treaty promulgated by law or by a binding decision of the United Nations Security Council, or before the Court of Justice of the European Union, shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).

(3) * The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and may be waived in cases deserving special consideration – with respect to the perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1) and (2) if they report the criminal offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and disclose the circumstances of the commission.

## 296. §

(1) * Whoever, in order not to exercise their legal rights or not to fulfill their obligations in judicial, arbitration or official proceedings, requests an undue advantage, accepts the undue advantage or its promise, or agrees with the requester or acceptor of an undue advantage requested by or given or promised to a third person with regard to them, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one year to five years for a felony.

(2) Whoever commits the criminal offence defined therein in the course of or in connection with proceedings pending before an international criminal court established by an international treaty promulgated by law or by a binding decision of the United Nations Security Council, or before the Court of Justice of the European Union, shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).

(3) * The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and may be waived in cases deserving special consideration – with respect to the perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1) and (2) if they report the criminal offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority, hand over the received undue pecuniary advantage or its equivalent value to the authority, and disclose the circumstances of the commission.

## 297. § *

## 298. § (1) Whoever

a) to a person who claims to influence an official person, or

b) * with regard to a person who claims to influence an official person, gives or promises an undue advantage to another person, shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding three years for a felony.

(1a) * Whoever gives or promises an undue advantage to a person posing as an official person or to another person with regard to them shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).

(2) * Whoever commits the criminal offence defined in paragraph (1) in connection with a person performing activities for or in the interest of a business organization shall be punishable by imprisonment not exceeding two years for a misdemeanor.

(3) * Whoever commits the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1) and (1a) in connection with a foreign official person shall be punishable pursuant to paragraph (1).

(4) * The punishment may be reduced without limitation – and may be waived in cases deserving special consideration – with respect to the perpetrator of the criminal offence defined in paragraphs (1)-(2) if they report the criminal offence to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and disclose the circumstances of the commission.

## 299. §

* (1) * Whoever, claiming to influence an official person, requests unlawful advantage for himself or another, accepts the unlawful advantage or its promise, or agrees with the requester or acceptor of the unlawful advantage requested by or given or promised to a third party in respect of him, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the offender

a) claims or creates the appearance that he bribes an official person,

b) impersonates an official person, or

c) commits the criminal offense professionally.

(2a) * Shall be punishable according to paragraph (2) whoever, impersonating an official person, requests unlawful advantage in connection with this simulated activity, accepts the unlawful advantage or its promise.

(3) Whoever commits the criminal offense defined in paragraph (1) in connection with a person acting for or in the interest of an economic organization shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.

(4) The punishment shall be imprisonment up to three years for a felony if the criminal offense defined in paragraph (3) is committed in connection with a person authorized to take independent measures who is acting for or in the interest of an economic organization.

(5) Shall be punishable according to paragraphs (1) and (2) whoever commits the criminal offense defined therein in connection with a foreign official person.

(6) The punishment may be reduced without limitation – in a case deserving special consideration it may be dispensed with – against the perpetrator of the criminal offense defined in paragraphs (1) and (3), if he reports the criminal offense to the authority before it came to the knowledge of the authority, hands over the received unlawful pecuniary advantage or its equivalent value to the authority, and discloses the circumstances of commission.

## 300. §

* (1) An official person who, in this capacity, obtains credible knowledge that bribery, acceptance of bribery, official bribery, acceptance of official bribery, bribery in judicial or official proceedings, acceptance of bribery in judicial or official proceedings, purchase of influence, or trading in influence has been committed that has not yet been uncovered, and does not report this to the authority as soon as possible, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) A relative of the offender shall not be punishable for failure to report a corruption criminal offense. 300/A. § * (1) For the purposes of this Chapter, breach of duty also includes the performance of a duty tied to the giving of an advantage.

(2) For the purposes of Sections 290 and 291, a foreign economic organization is an organization that has legal personality under its personal law and is authorized to conduct economic activity in the given organizational form.

## 301. §

(1) An official person who physically abuses another in the course of his proceedings shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the criminal offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a group.

(3) Whoever commits preparation for the criminal offense of abuse in official proceedings shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.

(4) * The punishment of a person who discloses the circumstances of committing the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) before the authority prior to the indictment may be reduced without limitation.

## 302. §

(1) A person performing a public duty who physically abuses another in the course of performing his public duty shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the criminal offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a group.

(3) Whoever commits preparation for the criminal offense of abuse in proceedings by a person performing a public duty shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.

(4) * The punishment of a person who discloses the circumstances of committing the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) before the authority prior to the indictment may be reduced without limitation.

## 303. §

(1) An official person who, in order to make another give or not give testimony or a statement, uses violence, threat, or other similar method shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the criminal offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a group.

(3) Whoever commits preparation for coerced confession shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.

(4) * The punishment of a person who discloses the circumstances of committing the criminal offense defined in paragraph (2) before the authority prior to the indictment may be reduced without limitation.

## 304. §

(1) An official person who unlawfully deprives another of his personal liberty in the course of his proceedings shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the unlawful detention is committed

a) from a base motive or for a base purpose,

b) with ill-treatment of the victim, or

c) causing serious consequence.

## 305. § An official person who, in order to cause unlawful disadvantage or obtain unlawful advantage,

a) breaches his official duty,

b) exceeds his official authority, or

c) otherwise abuses his official position, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

## 306. § A person performing a public duty who, in order to cause unlawful disadvantage or obtain unlawful advantage,

a) breaches his duty related to public duty,

b) exceeds his authority related to public duty, or

c) otherwise abuses his public duty position, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

## 307. § (1) * An official person who

a) conducts covert information gathering subject to the authorization of a judge or the minister responsible for justice without authorization, or applies a covert device subject to judicial authorization without authorization, or exceeds the limits of the authorization for these,

b) unlawfully orders or authorizes covert information gathering subject to the authorization of a judge or the minister responsible for justice, or the application of a covert device subject to judicial authorization, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) * Shall be punishable according to paragraph (1) an official person on the basis of whose false statement of fact covert information gathering subject to the authorization of a judge or the minister responsible for justice, or the application of a covert device subject to judicial authorization is ordered or authorized.

(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the criminal offense defined in paragraphs (1)–(2) causes significant violation of interests.

## 308. § (1) An official person who conducts a reliability investigation

a) * without prosecutorial approval, or exceeds the limits of the approval,

b) unlawfully approves, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) Shall be punishable according to paragraph (1) an official person who states a false fact in the decision ordering the reliability investigation, and on this basis the person authorized to do so approves the ordering of the reliability investigation.

(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the criminal offense defined in paragraphs (1)–(2) causes significant violation of interests.

## 309. §

The provisions of this Chapter shall also be applied accordingly to a member of a foreign state's authority performing judicial or law enforcement duties who is acting on the territory of Hungary pursuant to law.


This excerpt sits under: The legal regulation in its state of force as of today ( 2024.02.14. ).

## 310. § (1) Whoever against an official or foreign official person

a) obstructs by force or threat in their lawful proceedings,

b) compels by force or threat to take action in their lawful proceedings, or

c) maltreats during or because of their proceedings, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the violence against an official person is committed in a group, armed or in an armed manner.

(3) The organizer or leader of the group defined in paragraph (2) shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.

(4) Whoever participates in a group aimed at committing violence against an official person shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years, the organizer and leader of the group for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(5) Whoever maltreats an official person or foreign official person because of their proceedings shall be punishable according to paragraphs (1)–(4) even if the maltreated person is no longer an official person or foreign official person at the time of committing the offense.

(6) Whoever commits preparation aimed at violence against an official person shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.

(7) The participant of the group shall not be punishable under paragraph (4) if they leave the group voluntarily or at the request of the authority.

## 311. §

Shall be punishable according to § 310 whoever commits the offense defined therein against a person performing a public duty.

## 312. §

Shall be punishable according to § 310 whoever commits the offense defined therein against a person who acts in support or defense of an official person, foreign official person, or person performing a public duty.

## 313. § (1) Whoever against an internationally protected person staying in the country

a) maltreats,

b) deprives of personal liberty, or

c) endangers, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) Whoever threatens to commit the offense defined in paragraph (1) shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.

(3) Whoever commits preparation aimed at violence against an internationally protected person shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.

## 314. § (1) Whoever for the purpose of

a) compelling a state body, another state, or international organization to do, not do, or tolerate something,

b) intimidating the population,

c) changing or disrupting the constitutional, social, or economic order of another state, or disrupting the functioning of an international organization, commits a violent, public danger, or weapons-related offense against persons as defined in paragraph (4), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment.

(2) * Shall be punishable according to paragraph (1) whoever

a) for the purpose defined in paragraph (1) point a) seizes significant material assets and makes leaving them intact or their return dependent on the fulfillment of a demand addressed to a state body or international organization, or

b) * organizes or directs a terrorist group.

(3) The punishment may be mitigated without limit for whoever

a) ceases the terrorist act defined in paragraph (1) or (2) before serious consequences would arise from it, and

b) discloses their activity to the authority, if thereby they cooperate in preventing or mitigating the consequences of the offense, in detecting further perpetrators, or in preventing further offenses.

(4) For the purposes of this §, violent, public danger, or weapons-related offenses against persons are

a) homicide [§ 160 paragraphs (1)–(2)], bodily injury [§ 164 paragraphs (2)–(6) and (8)], intentional endangerment committed in the course of occupation [§ 165 paragraph (3)],

b) kidnapping [§ 190 paragraphs (1)–(4)], violation of personal liberty (§ 194),

c) offense against the safety of transport [§ 232 paragraphs (1)–(2)], endangerment of rail, air, or water transport [§ 233 paragraphs (1)–(2)],

d) abuse of radioactive material [§ 250 paragraphs (1)–(2)],

e) violence against an official person [§ 310 paragraphs (1)–(5)], violence against a person performing a public duty (§ 311), violence against a supporter of an official person or person performing a public duty (§ 312), violence against an internationally protected person [§ 313 paragraph (1)],

f) seizure of a vehicle [§ 320 paragraphs (1)–(2)], causing public danger [§ 322 paragraphs (1)–(3)], disruption of the operation of a public utility [§ 323 paragraphs (1)–(3)], abuse of explosives or explosive devices [§ 324 paragraphs (1)–(2)], abuse of firearms or ammunition [§ 325 paragraphs (1)–(3)],

g) abuse of weapons prohibited by international treaty [§ 326 paragraphs (1)–(5)], abuse of military technical products or services [§ 329 paragraphs (1)–(3)], abuse of dual-use products [§ 330 paragraphs (1)–(2)],

h) robbery [§ 365 paragraphs (1)–(4)] and vandalism [§ 371 paragraphs (1)–(6)],

i) * fraud committed using an information system [§ 375 paragraphs (2)–(4)], violation of an information system or data [§ 423 paragraphs (1)–(4)].

## 315. §

(1) * Whoever incites, offers, undertakes, agrees in joint commission, or provides the necessary or facilitating conditions for the purpose of facilitating the commission of the felony defined in § 314 paragraphs (1) or (2), shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) * Whoever carries out the acts defined in paragraph (1) for the purpose of committing the felony defined in § 314 paragraphs (1) or (2) in a terrorist group, shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.

(3) Shall not be punishable whoever reports the offense defined in paragraph (1) or (2) to the authority before it comes to the knowledge of the authority and discloses the circumstances of the commission.

## 316. §

* Whoever threatens to commit a terrorist act shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.

316/A. § * (1) Whoever

a) for the purpose of committing the felony defined in § 314 paragraphs (1) or (2), or inciting, offering, undertaking, agreeing in joint commission, or providing the necessary or facilitating conditions for the purpose of facilitating the commission, or

b) for the purpose of joining a terrorist group, enters the territory of Hungary, exits from it, or transits through it, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) Shall be punishable according to paragraph (1) whoever organizes the travel defined therein, or provides or collects material means for the purpose of supporting such travel.

## 317. §

Whoever obtains credible knowledge that a terrorist act is being prepared and does not report it to the authority as soon as possible shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

## 318. § * (1) Anyone who

a) provides or collects material means to ensure the conditions for a terrorist act,

b) supports with material means a person preparing to commit a terrorist act, or the perpetrator of a terrorist act, or another person with regard to these persons, or

c) provides or collects material means for the purpose of supporting the persons specified in point b), shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years for a felony.

(2) Anyone who commits the offense defined in paragraph (1) for the commission of a terrorist act in a terrorist group or in the interest of a member of a terrorist group, or supports the activity of the terrorist group by other means, or provides or collects material means for the purpose of such support of the terrorist group, shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years.

318/A. § * (1) Anyone who

a) provides or collects material means to ensure the conditions for a terrorist-type offense,

b) supports with material means a person preparing to commit a terrorist-type offense, or the perpetrator of a terrorist-type offense, or another person with regard to these persons, or

c) provides or collects material means for the purpose of supporting the persons specified in point b), shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years for a felony.

(2) For the purposes of paragraph (1), a terrorist-type offense is:

- a) homicide [Section 160 paragraph (1), Section 160 paragraph (2), if the act is directed against a person at an airport serving international civil aviation or on board an aircraft in flight or on a ship navigating at sea, or against an internationally protected person],
- b) bodily injury (Section 164, if the act is directed against a person at an airport serving international civil aviation or on board an aircraft in flight or on a ship navigating at sea or against an internationally protected person),
- c) kidnapping [Section 190 paragraphs (1)–(4)],
- d) offense against the safety of transport (Section 232, if the act is directed against an aircraft or a ship navigating at sea).
- e) misuse of radioactive material (Section 250),
- f) sabotage (Section 257),
- g) violence against an internationally protected person (Section 313),
- h) hijacking of a vehicle (Section 320),
- i) causing public danger [Section 322 paragraphs (1)–(3)],
- j) disruption of the operation of a public utility (Section 323),
- k) misuse of explosives or explosive devices (Section 324, if the act is committed on the premises of a public utility, or a public building or structure),
- l) misuse of firearms or ammunition (Section 325, if the act is committed on the premises of a public utility, or a public building or structure).

318/B. § *

## 319. §

* (1) For the purposes of Sections 314–315, Section 316/A and Section 318, a terrorist group is a group consisting of three or more persons, organized for a longer period, operating in a coordinated manner, whose purpose is to commit a terrorist act.

(2) For the purposes of Section 316/A, Section 318 and Section 318/A, material means shall mean the means, legal documents and instruments defined in Article 1(1) of Council Regulation (EC) No 2580/2001 of 27 December 2001 on specific restrictive measures directed against certain persons and entities with a view to combating terrorism.

## 320. §

(1) Anyone who seizes control of an aircraft, a mass transport vehicle or a vehicle suitable for mass freight transport by force, by threat, or by rendering another person unconscious or unable to defend themselves, shall be punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years for a felony.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment if the offense causes death.

(3) Anyone who commits preparation aimed at hijacking a vehicle shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(4) The punishment may be mitigated without limitation for anyone who abandons the offense before serious consequences would have resulted from it.

## 321. §

(1) Anyone who incites, offers, undertakes, agrees on joint commission of an offense in a criminal organization, or provides the necessary conditions or conditions facilitating this for the purpose of facilitating the commission, or supports the activity of the criminal organization by other means, shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years for a felony.

(2) Anyone who reports the offense to the authorities before it came to their knowledge and discloses the circumstances of the commission shall not be punishable for participation in a criminal organization.

## 322. §

(1) Anyone who causes public danger by triggering the destructive effect of matter or energy, or obstructs the prevention of public danger or the mitigation of its consequences, shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years for a felony.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the offense is committed

a) in a group,

b) causing particularly large damage or damage exceeding this, or

c) in conspiracy.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment if the offense causes death.

(4) Anyone who commits preparation aimed at causing public danger shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years.

(5) Anyone who commits causing public danger by negligence shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years for a misdemeanor, from one to five years in case of particularly large damage or damage exceeding this, and from two to eight years in case of causing death.

(6) The punishment may be mitigated without limitation for anyone who voluntarily eliminates the public danger before harmful consequences would have resulted from it.

322/A. § * (1) Anyone who

a) obstructs epidemiological isolation, observation, quarantine or control ordered for the purpose of preventing the introduction or spread of an infectious disease subject to quarantine obligation,

b) obstructs epidemiological isolation, observation, quarantine or control ordered during an epidemic,

c) obstructs the implementation of plant health or animal epidemic measures ordered for the purpose of preventing the introduction and removal, as well as the spread, or the elimination of the occurrence of infectious animal diseases or plant quarantine pests, shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years for a felony.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the offense is committed in a group.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the offense causes death.

(4) Anyone who commits preparation aimed at obstructing epidemiological measures shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to one year.

## 323. §

(1) Anyone who disrupts the operation of a public utility to a significant extent shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years for a felony.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the offense is committed

a) in a group,

b) in conspiracy, or

c) causing particularly large damage.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the offense is committed

a) armed,

b) in armed formation, or

c) causing particularly significant damage.

(4) Anyone who commits preparation aimed at disrupting the operation of a public utility shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to two years for a misdemeanor.

(5) Anyone who commits the offense by negligence shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years for a misdemeanor, or from one to five years in case of causing particularly large damage or damage exceeding this.

## 324. § (1) Anyone who

a) without authorization manufactures, acquires, possesses, places on the market explosive material, explosive device or equipment for their use, or transfers it to a person not entitled to possess them,

b) without authorization or exceeding the scope of the authorization brings it into the territory of the country, takes it out from there, or transports it through it, shall be punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years for a felony.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the offense is committed professionally or in conspiracy.

(3) Anyone who commits preparation aimed at misuse of explosives or explosive devices shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years.

## 325. § (1) Aki

a) * acquires or possesses an operational firearm without authorization,

b) manufactures or places into circulation a firearm without authorization,

c) * brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, or transports through it an operational firearm without authorization or exceeding the scope of the authorization,

d) * exceeds the scope of the authorization relating to the manufacture, trading, or the acquisition or possession of an operational firearm,

e) transfers a firearm held with authorization to a person not having authorization, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) Punishable according to paragraph (1) is a person who

a) acquires or possesses ammunition for a handgun, hunting firearm or sport firearm exceeding a small quantity or ammunition for another firearm without authorization,

b) manufactures or places into circulation ammunition without authorization,

c) brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, or transports through it ammunition without authorization or exceeding the scope of the authorization,

d) exceeds the scope of the authorization relating to the manufacture, acquisition, possession or trading of ammunition,

e) transfers to a person not having authorization ammunition for a handgun, hunting firearm or sport firearm held with authorization exceeding a small quantity or ammunition for another firearm.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the offense is committed professionally or in a criminal organization.

(4) A person who brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, or transports through it a handgun, hunting firearm or sport firearm held with authorization, or the ammunition belonging to it, without notification, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

(5) * A person who commits preparation for the offense defined in paragraphs (1)–(3) shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment for up to three years.

(6) For the purposes of this Section, a small quantity means a maximum of ten pieces of ammunition.

## 326. § (1) Aki nemzetközi szerződés által tiltott fegyvert

a) develops, manufactures,

b) acquires, possesses, uses, unlawfully neutralizes,

c) transfers to a person not authorized to possess it, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, transports through it, or places into circulation, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) Punishable according to paragraph (1) is a person who, without authorization or in deviation from the authorization, constructs or operates a facility suitable for the production of weapons prohibited by international treaty, or converts an existing facility for the purpose of manufacturing such weapons.

(3) Punishable by imprisonment from five to ten years is a person who

a) provides technical support for the development, manufacture, assembly, quality testing, operation, maintenance, or repair of weapons prohibited by international treaty,

b) deceives the authority or person entitled to make a decision for the purpose of obtaining the authorization specified by law for the operation of a facility suitable for the production of weapons prohibited by international treaty.

(4) The punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years if the offense defined in paragraph (3) is committed

a) professionally,

b) in a criminal organization, or

c) as an official person.

(5) Punishable by imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment is a person who

a) commits the offense defined in paragraph (1) professionally, or

b) commits the offense defined in paragraphs (1)–(2) in a criminal organization or as an official person.

(6) A person who commits preparation for abuse of weapons prohibited by international treaty shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.

(7) A person who commits the offense described in paragraph (2) by negligence shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to two years.

## 327. §

(1) A person who violates

a) the obligation relating to the freezing of funds or economic resources, or

b) an economic, commercial or financial prohibition ordered in regulations adopted on the basis of Hungary's obligations under international law or on the basis of Articles 75 and 215 of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union or in regulations or decisions adopted on the basis of the authorization of these regulations, as well as in Council decisions adopted on the basis of Article 29 of the Treaty establishing the European Union, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the violation of the international economic prohibition is committed

a) in connection with trade in goods applicable during the execution of the death penalty, torture or other cruel, inhuman or degrading treatment or punishment,

b) with violence, or

c) as an official person.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years if the violation of the international economic prohibition is committed

a) in connection with trade in firearms, ammunition, explosives, explosive materials, devices for their use, or other products intended for military use,

b) armed, or

c) in a criminal organization.

(4) A person who commits preparation for the violation of an international economic prohibition shall be punishable by imprisonment for up to three years.

(5) * For the purposes of this Section – unless the law proclaiming the obligation or prohibition on the basis of international legal obligation provides otherwise –

a) funds means the assets defined in Article 1 point (l) of Council Regulation (EU) No 267/2012 of 23 March 2012 concerning restrictive measures against Iran and repealing Regulation (EU) No 961/2010 (hereinafter referred to in this paragraph as: Regulation 267/2012/EU),

b) freezing of funds means what is defined in Article 1 point (k) of Regulation 267/2012/EU,

c) economic resources means the assets defined in Article 1 point (h) of Regulation 267/2012/EU,

d) freezing of economic resources means the assets defined in Article 1 point (j) of Regulation 267/2012/EU,

e) * goods applicable during the death penalty, torture or other cruel, inhuman or degrading treatment or punishment means goods defined in Annex II of Regulation (EU) 2019/125 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 16 January 2019 concerning trade in certain goods which could be used for capital punishment, torture or other cruel, inhuman or degrading treatment or punishment (hereinafter referred to in this Chapter as: Regulation (EU) 2019/125 of the European Parliament and of the Council).

## 328. §

(1) A person who obtains credible knowledge that a violation of an international economic prohibition is being prepared, or that such an offense not yet uncovered has been committed, and does not report it to the authority as soon as possible, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) A relative of the perpetrator may not be punished for failure to report the violation of an international economic prohibition.

## 329. § (1) Whoever

a) without authorization or exceeding the scope of authorization manufactures, places on the market military technical products, or provides military technical services,

b) uses military technical products in a manner different from the authorization,

c) manufactures, acquires, uses, keeps, transfers, places on the market, brings into the territory of the country, takes out of it, or transports through it prohibited military technical products, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) Whoever shall be punishable according to subsection (1):

a) provides technical support in connection with the development, production, trade, maintenance, repair, detection, identification or proliferation of chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, other nuclear explosives, or missile technology equipment capable of delivering them,

b) provides technical assistance in connection with other military use, different from that set out in point a), in relation to a country subject to arms export restrictions binding on Hungary based on international commitments.

(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years if the criminal offense defined in subsection (1) is committed in a criminal organization or as a business.

(4) Whoever commits preparation for abuse of military technical products or services shall be punishable by imprisonment from one to five years.

(5) For the purposes of this Section:

a) authorization shall also mean the International Import Certificate and documents replacing them,

b) * military technical product shall also mean the goods defined in Annex III of Regulation (EU) 2019/125 of the European Parliament and of the Council,

c) * prohibited military technical product means: the goods defined in Annex II of Regulation (EU) 2019/125 of the European Parliament and of the Council.

## 330. § (1) Whoever

a) without authorization or exceeding the scope of authorization places dual-use products in foreign trade circulation, including their transfer within the customs territory of the European Union, or

b) uses dual-use products in a manner different from the authorization, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years if the criminal offense defined in subsection (1) is committed

a) * in connection with the transfer to or from the territory of Hungary to the customs territory of the European Union of chemical substances falling under the scope of Annex 1 of Act LXIII of 2020 on the promulgation of the amendment to the Annex of the Convention on the Prohibition of the Development, Production, Stockpiling and Use of Chemical Weapons and on their Destruction, signed in Paris on 13 January 1993, or

b) in relation to nuclear dual-use products.

(3) Whoever commits preparation for abuse of dual-use products shall be punishable by imprisonment up to three years.

(4) For the purposes of this Section:

a) authorization shall also mean the International Import Certificate and documents replacing them,

b) dual-use product means: the product defined in Article 2(1) of Regulation (EC) No 428/2009 setting up a Community regime for the control of exports, transfer, brokering and transit of dual-use items,

c) * the customs territory of the European Union means the territory defined in Article 4 of Regulation (EU) No 952/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 9 October 2013 laying down the Union Customs Code.

## 331. §

* (1) Whoever incites to war before a large public, or otherwise conducts war propaganda, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) Whoever shall be punishable according to subsection (1), if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, incites to the support of terrorism before a large public, or otherwise conducts propaganda supporting terrorism.

## 332. § * Whoever before a large public

a) against the Hungarian nation,

b) against any national, ethnic, racial, religious group, or a member thereof, or

c) against certain groups of the population, or members thereof – in particular on the basis of disability, gender identity, sexual orientation – incites to violence or hatred, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

## 333. §

* Whoever before a large public denies, questions, presents in a trivial light the fact of genocide or other crimes against humanity committed by national socialist or communist regimes, or seeks to justify them, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

## 334. §

Whoever before a large public uses insulting or degrading expressions towards Hungary's anthem, flag, coat of arms or the Holy Crown, or otherwise desecrates them, if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to one year.

## 335. §

* Whoever in a manner suitable for disturbing public order – in particular in a manner violating the human dignity or right to respect of the deceased of the victims of totalitarian regimes – distributes, uses before a large public, or displays publicly a swastika, SS badge, arrow cross, sickle and hammer, five-pointed red star or symbols depicting them,

a) distributes,

b) uses before a large public, or

c) displays publicly, if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by confinement.

## 336. §

Whoever before a large public, in a manner suitable for disturbing public order, incites to general disobedience against the order of an authority, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

## 337. §

* (1) Whoever at the scene of a public danger, before a large public, in connection with the public danger, states or spreads a false fact or distorts a true fact in such a way that is suitable for causing confusion or unrest in a larger group of people at the scene of the public danger, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) Whoever during a special legal order, before a large public, states or spreads a false fact or distorts a true fact in such a way that is suitable for hindering or frustrating the effectiveness of defense, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment from one to five years.

## 338. §

(1) Whoever states or spreads a false fact suitable for disturbing public order, or creates the appearance that an event involving public danger is threatening to occur, shall be punishable for a felony by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years if the threat of public danger has seriously disturbed public order.

## 339. §

(1) Whoever engages in such provocatively anti-social, violent behavior that is suitable for causing outrage or alarm in others, if a more serious criminal offense is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor by imprisonment up to two years.

(2) The punishment for a felony shall be imprisonment up to three years if hooliganism is committed

a) in a group,

b) seriously disturbing public order,

c) armed,

d) having armed oneself, or

e) * at a public event or assembly.

## 340. §

(1) * Anyone who resists with violence or threat against a lawful measure taken by the organizer or host of a public event or assembly for the purpose of maintaining order, if a more serious crime is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) If a more serious crime is not committed, anyone who, at a sporting event, unlawfully enters or stays in an area of the facility that is restricted from spectators or a specific group of spectators, or throws an object into this area that endangers the holding of the sporting event or the physical integrity of others, shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

(3) The punishment is imprisonment for up to three years for a felony if the breach of peace is committed

a) in a group,

b) armed,

c) equipped with weapons or

d) as a special recidivist,

e) * in violation of the restrictions ensuring the peaceful nature of the assembly under the act on the right of assembly.

(4) For the purposes of special recidivism, similar offenses are

a) bodily injury [Section 164, paragraphs (3)–(6) and (8)],

b) violence against an official [Section 310, paragraphs (1)–(5)], violence against a person performing public duties (Section 311), violence against a supporter of an official or person performing public duties (Section 312),

c) hooliganism (Section 339),

d) vandalism [Section 371, paragraphs (1)–(6)].

## 341. §

Banning may also be applied against the perpetrator of hooliganism and breach of peace.

## 342. § (1) Anyone who

a) prepares a false public document or falsifies the content of a public document,

b) uses a false, falsified or genuine public document issued in another person's name,

c) participates in having false data, facts or statements concerning the existence, change or termination of a right or obligation recorded in a public document, shall be punishable for a felony with imprisonment for up to three years.

(1a) * The offense under paragraph (1), point c) is not committed in relation to the registration of an address in the register containing the personal data and addresses of citizens, if it is done with the consent of the landlord or by registering in one's own property.

(2) Anyone who commits preparation for the forgery of public documents as defined in paragraph (1), points a) or b), shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment for up to one year.

(3) Anyone who commits the forgery of public documents as defined in paragraph (1), point c) by negligence, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with confinement.

## 343. § (1) An official who, by abusing their official authority,

a) prepares a false public document,

b) falsifies the content of a public document, or

c) falsely records an essential fact in a public document, shall be punishable for a felony with imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The provisions of this Section shall also be applied accordingly to a member of a foreign state's authority performing judicial or law enforcement duties who acts on the territory of Hungary under the law.

343/A. § * (1) Anyone who

a) issues a document that can be confused with a diploma certifying a qualification obtained in higher education or a certificate certifying a vocational qualification obtained in non-formal education or school-based vocational training, or

b) unlawfully confers ba) a diploma certifying a qualification, bb) an academic degree, bc) a title or bd) an abbreviation defined in the Act on National Higher Education or that can be confused with it, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Anyone who commits the offense defined in paragraph (1) in a criminal conspiracy or in a business-like manner, shall be punishable for a felony with imprisonment for up to three years.

## 344. § Anyone who, in relation to a security document defined in separate legislation,

a) prepares a false security document entitling entry,

b) falsifies the content of a security document entitling entry,

c) uses a false, falsified or genuine security document entitling entry issued in another person's name, if a more serious crime is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment for up to two years.

## 345. §

Anyone who uses a false, falsified or private document with false content to prove the existence, change or termination of a right or obligation, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment for up to one year.

## 346. §

* (1) Anyone who, in relation to one or more public documents that are not or not exclusively their own,

a) unlawfully obtains from another without their consent,

b) unlawfully destroys, damages, or

c) unlawfully takes from another or unlawfully conceals from the person entitled, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) If another crime is not committed, anyone who transfers or receives a public document for the purpose of unlawful gain shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

(3) Anyone who commits the offense defined in paragraph (1) in relation to a private document in order to obtain an unlawful advantage or cause an unlawful disadvantage, shall be punishable with imprisonment for up to one year.

## 347. § (1) Anyone who

a) removes or falsifies a unique identifier,

b) obtains, uses or possesses a thing whose unique identifier is false, falsified, or whose unique identifier has been removed, shall be punishable for a felony with imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the offense defined in paragraph (1) is committed in a business-like manner or in a criminal conspiracy.

(3) For the purposes of this Section, a unique identifier means: a marking applied by the manufacturer or the authority on a thing, or on its component, for the purpose of unique identification, where the possession or intended use of the thing is subject to official authorization by law.

## 348. §

Anyone who, for the purpose of unlawful gain, falsifies the value indicated by the odometer of a road motor vehicle by affecting the instrument or influencing the measurement process, or prevents the operation of the instrument, if a more serious crime is not committed, shall be punishable for a misdemeanor with imprisonment for up to one year.

## 349. § * (1) Anyone who

a) acquires ownership of land used for agricultural and forestry purposes,

b) establishes a usufruct right or right of use for land used for agricultural and forestry purposes by entering into a void contract by circumventing the statutory prohibition or restriction applicable thereto, shall be punishable for a felony with imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) Anyone who

a) acquires ownership of land used for agricultural and forestry purposes,

b) uses land used for agricultural and forestry purposes or collects its benefits based on an agreement created to conceal a void contract entered into by them by circumventing the statutory prohibition or restriction on the establishment of a usufruct right or right of use for land used for agricultural and forestry purposes, if a more serious crime is not committed, shall be punishable for a felony with imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) * A lawyer, chamber legal counsel or notary who participates in the conclusion of a contract under paragraph (1) shall be punishable according to paragraph (1).

(4) The punishment of the perpetrator of the offense under paragraphs (1) and (2) may be mitigated without limitation if they report the offense to the authority before it comes to the authority's knowledge and disclose the circumstances of the commission.

349/A. § * (1) Anyone who enters into an agreement as a result of which the result of a competition or match organized in the competition system of a sports association or otherwise appearing in the competition calendar of the sports association does not develop in accordance with the competition rules or the principles of fair play, if another crime is not committed, shall be punishable for a felony with imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from one to five years if the offense is committed in a criminal conspiracy or in a business-like manner.

## 350. §

* (1) Aki a választási eljárásról szóló törvény vagy a népszavazás kezdeményezéséről, az európai polgári kezdeményezésről, valamint a népszavazási eljárásról szóló törvény hatálya alá tartozó választás, népszavazás és európai polgári kezdeményezés során

a) a jelölési eljárás szabályait megszegve erőszakkal, fenyegetéssel, megtévesztéssel vagy anyagi juttatással szerez ajánlást,

b) népszavazás vagy európai polgári kezdeményezés indítványozása érdekében erőszakkal, fenyegetéssel, megtévesztéssel vagy anyagi juttatással szerez aláírást,

c) jogosultság nélkül szavaz,

d) jogosulatlanul aláír, hamis adatokat tüntet fel,

e) arra jogosultat a választásban, a népszavazásban, a népszavazási vagy az európai polgári kezdeményezésben akadályoz, vagy erőszakkal, fenyegetéssel, megtévesztéssel, vagy anyagi juttatással befolyásolni törekszik,

f) megsérti a választás vagy a népszavazás titkosságát,

g) meghamisítja a választás, a népszavazás, a népszavazási vagy az európai polgári kezdeményezés eredményét,

h) a jelölési eljárás során az ajánlásáért, illetve a népszavazás kezdeményezése vagy európai polgári kezdeményezés indítványozása során az aláírásáért anyagi juttatást fogad el,

i) szavazatát anyagi juttatás nyújtásától teszi függővé, és erre tekintettel anyagi juttatást fogad el, bűntettet követ el és három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

350/A. § * A választási eljárásról szóló törvény szerinti jelölő szervezet azon tagja, felelős személye vagy vezető tisztségviselője, valamint a választási eljárásról szóló törvény szerinti jelölt, aki tiltott külföldi támogatást, vagy e tilalom kijátszása érdekében a tiltott külföldi támogatás eredetét leplező megállapodásból származó vagyoni előnyt használ fel, bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

## 351. §

(1) Aki a bíróság által feloszlatott egyesület vezetésében részt vesz, ha súlyosabb bűncselekmény nem valósul meg, bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) Aki a bíróság által feloszlatott egyesület működésében a köznyugalom megzavarására alkalmas módon vesz részt, vétség miatt két évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(3) Aki a bíróság által feloszlatott egyesület vezetéséhez vagy működéséhez szükséges vagy azt könnyítő feltételeket biztosítja, vagy ahhoz anyagi eszközöket szolgáltat, egy évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

## 352. § Aki

a) olyan, a közbiztonság, közrend fenntartására irányuló tevékenységet szervez, amelyre jogszabály nem jogosítja fel, vagy

b) a közbiztonság, közrend fenntartásának látszatát keltő tevékenységet szervez, vétség miatt két évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

352/A. § * (1) Aki Magyarországnak az államhatár rendje védelmét biztosító létesítmény által védett területére a létesítményen keresztül jogosulatlanul belép, bűntett miatt három évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) A büntetés egy évtől öt évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha az (1) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekményt

a) fegyveresen,

b) felfegyverkezve,

c) tömegzavargás résztvevőjeként követik el.

(3) Aki az (1) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekményt fegyveresen vagy felfegyverkezve tömegzavargás résztvevőjeként követi el, két évtől nyolc évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(4) A büntetés öt évtől tíz évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha a (2) vagy (3) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekmény halált okoz. 352/B. § * (1) Aki az államhatár rendjének védelmét biztosító létesítményt, illetve eszközt megsemmisíti vagy megrongálja, ha súlyosabb bűncselekmény nem valósul meg, bűntett miatt egy évtől öt évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) A büntetés két évtől nyolc évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha az (1) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekményt

a) fegyveresen,

b) felfegyverkezve,

c) tömegzavargás résztvevőjeként követik el.

(3) Aki az (1) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekményt fegyveresen vagy felfegyverkezve tömegzavargás résztvevőjeként követi el, öt évtől tíz évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(4) A büntetés tíz évtől húsz évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha a (2) vagy (3) bekezdésben meghatározott bűncselekmény halált okoz.

352/C. § * Aki az államhatár rendjének védelmét biztosító létesítmény építésével vagy karbantartásával kapcsolatos munkavégzést akadályozza, ha más bűncselekmény nem valósul meg, vétség miatt egy évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

## 353. §

(1) * Aki államhatárnak más által a jogszabályi rendelkezések megszegésével történő átlépéséhez segítséget nyújt, bűntett miatt egy évtől öt évig terjedő szabadságvesztéssel büntetendő.

(2) * A büntetés két évtől nyolc évig terjedő szabadságvesztés, ha az embercsempészést

a) vagyoni haszonszerzés végett,

b) államhatár átlépéséhez több személynek segítséget nyújtva, vagy

c) az államhatár rendjének védelmét biztosító létesítmény, illetve eszköz megsemmisítésével vagy megrongálásával követik el.
